They contain information about terrain, resources, features, and other map elements.\n\n## File Structure\n\n### Basic Format\nCiv6Map files use a hierarchical structure with sections defined by tags:\n- Lines starting with specific keywords define different map elements\n- Coordinates are typically in (x, y) format\n- The map uses a hexagonal grid system\n\n### Common Sections\n\n#### Map Metadata\n```\nVersion=\nWidth=\nHeight=\n```\n\n#### Terrain Data\n```\nTerrainType [x] [y] [terrain_id]\n```\nCommon terrain types:\n- TERRAIN_GRASS\n- TERRAIN_PLAINS\n- TERRAIN_DESERT\n- TERRAIN_TUNDRA\n- TERRAIN_SNOW\n- TERRAIN_COAST\n- TERRAIN_OCEAN\n\n#### Features\n```\nFeatureType [x] [y] [feature_id]\n```\nCommon features:\n- FEATURE_FOREST\n- FEATURE_JUNGLE\n- FEATURE_MARSH\n- FEATURE_FLOODPLAINS\n- FEATURE_MOUNTAINS (not passable, provides adjacency)\n\n#### Resources\n```\nResourceType [x] [y] [resource_id]\n```\n\n## Python Parsing Example\n\n```python\nimport re\n\nclass Civ6MapParser:\n    def __init__(self, filepath):\n        self.filepath = filepath\n        self.width = 0\n        self.height = 0\n        self.terrain = {}\n        self.features = {}\n        self.resources = {}\n\n    def parse(self):\n        with open(self.filepath, 'r') as f:\n            for line in f:\n                line = line.strip()\n                if not line:\n                    continue\n\n                if line.startswith('Width'):\n                    self.width = int(line.split('=')[1])\n                elif line.startswith('Height'):\n                    self.height = int(line.split('=')[1])\n                elif line.startswith('TerrainType'):\n                    parts = line.split()\n                    x, y = int(parts[1]), int(parts[2])\n                    terrain = parts[3] if len(parts) > 3 else 'UNKNOWN'\n                    self.terrain[(x, y)] = terrain\n                elif line.startswith('FeatureType'):\n                    parts = line.split()\n                    x, y = int(parts[1]), int(parts[2])\n                    feature = parts[3] if len(parts) > 3 else 'UNKNOWN'\n                    self.features[(x, y)] = feature\n                elif line.startswith('ResourceType'):\n                    parts = line.split()\n                    x, y = int(parts[1]), int(parts[2])\n                    resource = parts[3] if len(parts) > 3 else 'UNKNOWN'\n                    self.resources[(x, y)] = resource\n\n    def get_tile_info(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Get all information about a specific tile\"\"\"\n        return {\n            'terrain': self.terrain.get((x, y), 'UNKNOWN'),\n            'feature': self.features.get((x, y), None),\n            'resource': self.resources.get((x, y), None)\n        }\n\n    def is_valid_tile(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Check if coordinates are within map bounds\"\"\"\n        return 0 <= x < self.width and 0 <= y < self.height\n```\n\n## Usage\n\n```python\n# Parse a map file\nparser = Civ6MapParser('/path/to/map.Civ6Map')\nparser.parse()\n\n# Get tile information\ntile_info = parser.get_tile_info(5, 10)\nprint(f\"Terrain: {tile_info['terrain']}\")\nprint(f\"Feature: {tile_info['feature']}\")\nprint(f\"Resource: {tile_info['resource']}\")\n\n# Check if tile is valid\nif parser.is_valid_tile(x, y):\n    print(\"Tile is within map bounds\")\n```\n\n## Notes\n- Civ6 uses a hexagonal grid, which affects adjacency calculations\n- Mountain tiles are impassable but provide adjacency bonuses\n- Water tiles (coast/ocean) have different placement rules\n- Some features like mountains cannot have districts placed on them\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":411,"cache_read_input_tokens":20402,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":411,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9dcd5f84-9050-4c07-9e38-c1ab54cbcda1","uuid":"19a2f309-84e7-4cfe-9b6c-ddd66268e537"}

line.split()\n                    x, y = int(parts[1]), int(parts[2])\n                    feature = parts[3] if len(parts) > 3 else 'UNKNOWN'\n                    self.features[(x, y)] = feature\n                elif line.startswith('ResourceType'):\n                    parts = line.split()\n                    x, y = int(parts[1]), int(parts[2])\n                    resource = parts[3] if len(parts) > 3 else 'UNKNOWN'\n                    self.resources[(x, y)] = resource\n\n    def get_tile_info(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Get all information about a specific tile\"\"\"\n        return {\n            'terrain': self.terrain.get((x, y), 'UNKNOWN'),\n            'feature': self.features.get((x, y), None),\n            'resource': self.resources.get((x, y), None)\n        }\n\n    def is_valid_tile(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Check if coordinates are within map bounds\"\"\"\n        return 0 <= x < self.width and 0 <= y < self.height\n```\n\n## Usage\n\n```python\n# Parse a map file\nparser = Civ6MapParser('/path/to/map.Civ6Map')\nparser.parse()\n\n# Get tile information\ntile_info = parser.get_tile_info(5, 10)\nprint(f\"Terrain: {tile_info['terrain']}\")\nprint(f\"Feature: {tile_info['feature']}\")\nprint(f\"Resource: {tile_info['resource']}\")\n\n# Check if tile is valid\nif parser.is_valid_tile(x, y):\n    print(\"Tile is within map bounds\")\n```\n\n## Notes\n- Civ6 uses a hexagonal grid, which affects adjacency calculations\n- Mountain tiles are impassable but provide adjacency bonuses\n- Water tiles (coast/ocean) have different placement rules\n- Some features like mountains cannot have districts placed on them\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Understanding these rules is crucial for optimal city planning.\n\n## District Types and Adjacency Rules\n\n### Campus (Science District)\n**Standard Adjacency:**\n- **+1** per adjacent Mountain tile\n- **+1** per adjacent Rainforest tile\n- **+1** per 2 adjacent Reef tiles\n\n**District Adjacency:**\n- **+1** per 2 adjacent district tiles (excluding City Center)\n\n**Geothermal Fissure/Reef:** +1 per tile\n\n### Holy Site (Religious District)\n**Standard Adjacency:**\n- **+1** per adjacent Mountain tile\n- **+1** per adjacent Natural Wonder tile\n- **+1** per 2 adjacent Woods tiles\n\n**District Adjacency:**\n- **+1** per 2 adjacent district tiles (excluding City Center)\n\n### Theater Square (Culture District)\n**Standard Adjacency:**\n- **+1** per adjacent Wonder tile\n- **+1** per 2 adjacent district tiles (excluding City Center)\n\n**Entertainment/Water Park Adjacency:**\n- **+2** per adjacent Entertainment Complex or Water Park\n\n### Commercial Hub (Gold **+1** per 2 adjacent district tiles (excluding City Center)\n\n### Harbor (Maritime District)\n**Standard Adjacency:**\n- **+1** per adjacent Sea Resource tile\n- **+2** per adjacent City Center\n- **+1** per 2 adjacent district tiles (excluding City Center)\n\n### Industrial Zone (Production District)\n**Standard Adjacency:**\n- **+1** per adjacent Mine\n- **+1** per adjacent Quarry\n- **+1** per adjacent Lumber Mill\n- **+1** per 2 adjacent Strategic Resources\n- **+2** per adjacent Aqueduct, Dam, or Canal\n\n**District Adjacency:**\n- **+1** per 2 adjacent district tiles (excluding City Center)\n\n### Entertainment Complex\n**Standard Adjacency:**\n- **+1** per adjacent Entertainment Complex or Water Park\n- **+2** per adjacent Entertainment Complex (adjacency from another Entertainment Complex)\n\n### Encampment (Military District)\n- No adjacency bonuses\n\n### Neighborhood (Housing District)\n- No adjacency bonuses\n\n### Aqueduct\n- **+2** bonus to adjacent Industrial Zone\n- Must be adjacent to City Center and a water source (River, Lake, Mountain, or Oasis)\n\n## Important Rules\n\n### General Placement Rules\n1. Districts cannot be placed on:\n   - Mountains\n   - Natural Wonders\n   - Ocean tiles (except Harbor)\n   - Tiles with existing districts or city centers\n\n2. Districts must be within the workable range of a city (3 tiles from city center)\n\n3. Number of districts limited by population:\n   - Population 1: City Center only\n   - Population 4: +1 district\n   - Population 7: +2 districts\n   - Population 10: +3 districts\n   - And so on (+1 district per 3 population)\n\n### Hexagonal Grid Adjacency\nEach hex tile has up to 6 adjacent neighbors:\n- In a flat-top hex grid: NW, NE, E, SE, SW, W\n- Check all 6 directions for adjacency calculations\n\n## Python Implementation Example\n\n```python\nclass AdjacencyCalculator:\n    def __init__(self, map_data):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n\n    def get_neighbors(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Get all 6 hexagonal neighbors for even-q vertical layout\"\"\"\n        neighbors = []\n        if y % 2 == 0:  # Even column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, -1), (1, 0), (0, 1), (-1, 0), (-1, -1)]\n        else:  # Odd column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, 0), (1, 1), (0, 1), (-1, 1), (-1, 0)]\n\n        for dx, dy in offsets:\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            if self.map_data.is_valid_tile(nx, ny):\n                neighbors.append((nx, ny))\n        return neighbors\n\n    def calculate_campus_adjacency(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Calculate Campus adjacency bonus\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.get_neighbors(x, y)\n\n        district_count = 0\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Mountains: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Rainforest: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Count other districts (not City Center)\n            if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                district_count += 1\n\n        # +1 per 2 adjacent districts\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n\n        return bonus\n\n    def calculate_industrial_zone_adjacency(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Calculate Industrial Zone adjacency bonus\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.get_neighbors(x, y)\n\n        district_count = 0\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Check for mines, quarries (would need improvement data)\n            # Check for strategic resources\n\n            # Aqueduct bonus\n            if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] == 'AQUEDUCT':\n                bonus += 2\n\n            # Count other districts\n            if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] not in ['CITY_CENTER', 'AQUEDUCT']:\n                district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return bonus\n```\n\n## Maximization Strategies\n\n1. **Campus**: Place near mountain ranges or rainforest clusters\n2. **Holy Site**: Place near mountains and woods\n3. **Industrial Zone**: Group with Aqueducts for +2 bonus\n4. **Commercial Hub**: Place on rivers, near harbors\n5. **Harbor**: Place near sea resources and city center\n\n## Notes\n- Adjacency bonuses are evaluated at placement time\n- Some civilizations have unique district bonuses (e.g., Japan's +1 for all adjacent districts)\n- Natural Wonder adjacency provides significant bonuses\n- Strategic clustering of districts can create multiplicative bonuses\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":410,"cache_read_input_tokens":21990,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":410,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9dcd5f84-9050-4c07-9e38-c1ab54cbcda1","uuid":"7be14209-4551-401e-9005-96ade90c86b5"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01E6iiNCSMkGmSyx1Px9AJ6Y","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01Pr7MueN96ZxfePsRPbwLhD","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/hexagonal-grid-math.md","content":"# Hexagonal Grid Mathematics\n\n## Overview\nCivilization 6 uses a hexagonal grid system. Understanding hex grid math is essential for calculating adjacency, distance, and valid placements.\n\n## Coordinate Systems\n\n### Offset Coordinates (Used in Civ6)\nThere are several offset coordinate systems. Civ6 typically uses **even-q vertical layout**:\n- **q**: column (x-axis)\n- **r**: row (y-axis)\n- Even columns (q % 2 == 0) are offset downward\n\n### Neighbor Calculation\n\nIn an even-q vertical layout, each hex has 6 neighbors. The offset depends on whether the column is even or odd.\n\n```python\ndef get_hex_neighbors_evenq(x, y):\n    \"\"\"\n    Get all 6 neighbors for even-q vertical layout\n    Returns list of (x, y) tuples\n    \"\"\"\n    if y % 2 == 0:  # Even column\n        # Neighbors for even columns\n        offsets = [\n            (0, -1),   # North\n            (1, -1),   # Northeast\n            (1, 0),    # Southeast\n            (0, 1),    # South\n            (-1, 0),   # Southwest\n            (-1, -1)   # Northwest\n        ]\n    else:  # Odd column\n        # Neighbors for odd columns\n        offsets = [\n            (0, -1),   # North\n            (1, 0),    # Northeast\n            (1, 1),    # Southeast\n            (0, 1),    # South\n            (-1, 1),   # Southwest\n            (-1, 0)    # Northwest\n        ]\n\n    neighbors = []\n    for dx, dy in offsets:\n        neighbors.append((x + dx, y + dy))\n\n    return neighbors\n```\n\n## Alternative: Axial Coordinates\n\nAxial coordinates (q, r) are sometimes easier for calculations:\n- q: column axis\n- r: diagonal axis\n\n```python\ndef offset_to_axial(col, row):\n    \"\"\"Convert offset coordinates to axial\"\"\"\n    q = col\n    r = row - (col - (col & 1)) // 2\n    return (q, r)\n\ndef axial_to_offset(q, r):\n    \"\"\"Convert axial coordinates to offset\"\"\"\n    col = q\n    row = r + (q - (q & 1)) // 2\n    return (col, row)\n```\n\n## Distance Calculation\n\n### Manhattan Distance on Hex Grid\n\n```python\ndef hex_distance_offset(x1, y1, x2, y2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate hex distance using offset coordinates\"\"\"\n    # Convert to axial\n    q1, r1 = offset_to_axial(x1, y1)\n    q2, r2 = offset_to_axial(x2, y2)\n\n    # Axial distance formula\n    return (abs(q1 - q2) + abs(q1 + r1 - q2 - r2) + abs(r1 - r2)) // 2\n\ndef hex_distance_axial(q1, r1, q2, r2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate hex distance using axial coordinates\"\"\"\n    return (abs(q1 - q2) + abs(q1 + r1 - q2 - r2) + abs(r1 - r2)) // 2\n```\n\n## Range Calculations\n\n### Get All Tiles Within Range\n\n```python\ndef get_tiles_in_range(center_x, center_y, radius, max_x, max_y):\n    \"\"\"\n    Get all tiles within a certain radius of a center tile\n    Useful for determining workable city tiles (radius 3 in Civ6)\n    \"\"\"\n    tiles = []\n\n    # Convert center to axial\n    center_q, center_r = offset_to_axial(center_x, center_y)\n\n    # Iterate through potential tiles\n    for q in range(-radius, radius + 1):\n        for r in range(max(-radius, -q - radius), min(radius, -q + radius) + 1):\n            # Calculate actual tile position\n            tile_q = center_q + q\n            tile_r = center_r + r\n\n            # Convert back to offset\n            tile_x, tile_y = axial_to_offset(tile_q, tile_r)\n\n            # Check bounds\n            if 0 <= tile_x < max_x and 0 <= tile_y < max_y:\n                tiles.append((tile_x, tile_y))\n\n    return tiles\n```\n\n## Complete Helper Class\n\n```python\nclass HexGrid:\n    def __init__(self, width, height):\n        self.width = width\n        self.height = height\n\n    def is_valid(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Check if coordinates are within bounds\"\"\"\n        return 0 <= x < self.width and 0 <= y < self.height\n\n    def get_neighbors(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Get valid neighbors for a tile\"\"\"\n        if y % 2 == 0:\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, -1), (1, 0), (0, 1), (-1, 0), (-1, -1)]\n        else:\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, 0), (1, 1), (0, 1), (-1, 1), (-1, 0)]\n\n        neighbors = []\n        for dx, dy in offsets:\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            if self.is_valid(nx, ny):\n                neighbors.append((nx, ny))\n\n        return neighbors\n\n    def get_tiles_in_range(self, center_x, center_y, radius):\n        \"\"\"Get all valid tiles within radius of center\"\"\"\n        tiles = set()\n        tiles.add((center_x, center_y))\n\n        # BFS approach\n        current_ring = {(center_x, center_y)}\n\n        for _ in range(radius):\n            next_ring = set()\n            for tile_x, tile_y in current_ring:\n                for neighbor in self.get_neighbors(tile_x, tile_y):\n                    if neighbor not in tiles:\n                        next_ring.add(neighbor)\n                        tiles.add(neighbor)\n            current_ring = next_ring\n\n        return list(tiles)\n\n    def distance(self, x1, y1, x2, y2):\n        \"\"\"Calculate distance between two tiles\"\"\"\n        # Simple BFS approach for accuracy\n        if (x1, y1) == (x2, y2):\n            return 0\n\n        visited = {(x1, y1)}\n        queue = [(x1, y1, 0)]\n\n        while queue:\n            x, y, dist = queue.pop(0)\n            for nx, ny in self.get_neighbors(x, y):\n                if (nx, ny) == (x2, y2):\n                    return dist + 1\n                if (nx, ny) not in visited:\n                    visited.add((nx, ny))\n                    queue.append((nx, ny, dist + 1))\n\n        return -1  # Not reachable\n```\n\n## Usage Example\n\n```python\n# Create hex grid\ngrid = HexGrid(20, 20)\n\n# Get neighbors of a tile\nneighbors = grid.get_neighbors(5, 10)\nprint(f\"Tile (5, 10) has {len(neighbors)} neighbors\")\n\n# Get all tiles within range 3 (workable city tiles in Civ6)\nworkable_tiles = grid.get_tiles_in_range(10, 10, 3)\nprint(f\"City at (10, 10) can work {len(workable_tiles)} tiles\")\n\n# Calculate distance\ndist = grid.distance(0, 0, 5, 5)\nprint(f\"Distance from (0, 0) to (5, 5): {dist}\")\n```\n\n## Common Patterns in Civ6\n\n### City Workable Range\nCities can work tiles within 3 hexes of the city center.\n\n### District Placement\nDistricts must be placed within the workable range of a city.\n\n### Adjacency Checks\nAlways check all 6 neighbors when calculating adjacency bonuses.\n\n## Notes\n- Civ6 maps can use different coordinate systems depending on version\n- Always verify the coordinate system by testing with known positions\n- Edge cases occur at map boundaries - always validate coordinates\n- Some Civ6 maps wrap horizontally (cylindrical maps) - handle carefully\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":444,"cache_read_input_tokens":24183,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":444,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9dcd5f84-9050-4c07-9e38-c1ab54cbcda1","uuid":"4c5353da-2db8-44c8-abbd-aa8ccbcb25de"}

Civ6 typically uses **even-q vertical layout**:\n- **q**: column (x-axis)\n- **r**: row (y-axis)\n- Even columns (q % 2 == 0) are offset downward\n\n### Neighbor Calculation\n\nIn an even-q vertical layout, each hex has 6 neighbors. The offset depends on whether the column is even or odd.\n\n```python\ndef get_hex_neighbors_evenq(x, y):\n    \"\"\"\n    Get all 6 neighbors for even-q vertical layout\n    Returns list of (x, y) tuples\n    \"\"\"\n    if y % 2 == 0:  # Even column\n        # Neighbors for even columns\n        offsets = [\n            (0, -1),   # North\n            (1, -1),   # Northeast\n            (1, 0),    # Southeast\n            (0, 1),    # Northeast\n            (1, 1),    # Southeast\n            (0, 1),    # South\n            (-1, 1),   # Southwest\n            (-1, 0)    # Northwest\n        ]\n\n    neighbors = []\n    for dx, dy in offsets:\n        neighbors.append((x + dx, y + dy))\n\n    return neighbors\n```\n\n## Alternative: Axial Coordinates\n\nAxial coordinates (q, r) are sometimes easier for calculations:\n- q: column axis\n- r: diagonal axis\n\n```python\ndef offset_to_axial(col, row):\n    \"\"\"Convert offset coordinates to axial\"\"\"\n    q = col\n    r = row - (col - (col & 1)) // 2\n    return (q, r)\n\ndef axial_to_offset(q, r):\n    \"\"\"Convert axial coordinates to offset\"\"\"\n    col = q\n    row = r + (q - (q & 1)) // 2\n    return (col, row)\n```\n\n## Distance Calculation\n\n### Manhattan Distance on Hex Grid\n\n```python\ndef hex_distance_offset(x1, y1, x2, y2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate hex distance using offset coordinates\"\"\"\n    # Simple BFS approach for accuracy\n        if (x1, y1) == (x2, y2):\n            return 0\n\n        visited = {(x1, y1)}\n        queue = [(x1, y1, 0)]\n\n        while queue:\n            x, y, dist = queue.pop(0)\n            for nx, ny in self.get_neighbors(x, y):\n                if (nx, ny) == (x2, y2):\n                    return dist + 1\n                if (nx, ny) not in visited:\n                    visited.add((nx, ny))\n                    queue.append((nx, ny, dist + 1))\n\n        return -1  # Given constraints (map size, number of cities, population limits), find the placement that maximizes total adjacency bonuses.\n\n## Problem Formulation\n\n### Decision Variables\n- City center location(s): (x, y) coordinates\n- District types: Which districts to build\n- District placements: (x, y) coordinates for each district\n\n### Constraints\n1. **Population limits**: Number of districts ≤ f(population)\n   - Pop 1-3: 0 districts\n   - Pop 4-6: 1 district\n   - Pop 7-9: 2 districts\n   - Pop 10-12: 3 districts\n\n2. **Placement validity**:\n   - Within 3 tiles of city center\n   - Valid terrain (not mountains, not ocean except harbor)\n   - No overlapping districts\n\n3. **Map boundaries**: All placements within map dimensions\n\n### Objective\nMaximize: Σ(adjacency_bonus(district_i))\n\n## Optimization Approaches\n\n### 1. Exhaustive Search (Small Spaces)\nFor small problems, enumerate all valid combinations.\n\n```python\ndef exhaustive_search(map_data, city_center, max_districts, district_types):\n    \"\"\"\n    Try all valid combinations of district placements\n    \"\"\"\n    workable_tiles = get_tiles_in_range(city_center, 3)\n    valid_tiles = [t for t in workable_tiles if is_valid_for_district(t)]\n\n    best_solution = None\n    best_score = -1\n\n    # For each combination of tiles\n    from itertools import combinations\n    for tiles in combinations(valid_tiles, max_districts):\n        # For each permutation of district types\n        from itertools import permutations\n        for district_assignment in permutations(district_types, max_districts):\n            placements = dict(zip(tiles, district_assignment))\n            score = calculate_total_adjacency(placements)\n\n            if score > best_score:\n                best_score = score\n                best_solution = placements\n\n    return best_solution, best_score\n```\n\n### 2. Greedy Algorithm\nBuild districts one at a time, always choosing the placement with highest marginal benefit.\n\n```python\ndef greedy_placement(map_data, city_center, districts_to_build):\n    \"\"\"\n    Greedy algorithm: place each district where it gets best bonus\n    \"\"\"\n    placements = {}\n    workable_tiles = get_tiles_in_range(city_center, 3)\n\n    for district_type in districts_to_build:\n        best_tile = None\n        best_bonus = -1\n\n        for tile in workable_tiles:\n            if is_valid_placement(tile, placements):\n                # Calculate bonus if we place district here\n                bonus = calculate_district_adjacency(\n                    district_type, tile, placements, map_data\n                )\n\n                if bonus > best_bonus:\n                    best_bonus = bonus\n                    best_tile = tile\n\n        if best_tile:\n            placements[best_tile] = district_type\n\n    return placements\n```\n\n### 3. Hill Climbing / Local Search\nStart with random/greedy solution, iteratively improve by swapping.\n\n```python\ndef hill_climbing(map_data, city_center, initial_placements, max_iterations=1000):\n    \"\"\"\n    Hill climbing: start with solution, iteratively swap to improve\n    \"\"\"\n    current = initial_placements.copy()\n    current_score = calculate_total_adjacency(current)\n\n    workable_tiles = get_tiles_in_range(city_center, 3)\n\n    for _ in range(max_iterations):\n        improved = False\n\n        # Try swapping each district to a different tile\n        for tile, district in list(current.items()):\n            for new_tile in workable_tiles:\n                if new_tile not in current and is_valid_placement(new_tile):\n                    # Try the swap\n                    test = current.copy()\n                    del test[tile]\n                    test[new_tile] = district\n\n                    test_score = calculate_total_adjacency(test)\n                    if test_score > current_score:\n                        current = test\n                        current_score = test_score\n                        improved = True\n                        break\n\n            if improved:\n                break\n\n        if not improved:\n            break  # Local optimum found\n\n    return current, current_score\n```\n\n### 4. Dynamic Programming (Limited Cases)\nFor certain structured problems, DP can find optimal solutions.\n\n```python\ndef dp_optimization(map_data, city_center, districts, max_districts):\n    \"\"\"\n    Dynamic programming approach for district placement\n    State: (tiles_used, districts_placed)\n    \"\"\"\n    workable_tiles = get_tiles_in_range(city_center, 3)\n    valid_tiles = [t for t in workable_tiles if is_valid_for_district(t)]\n\n    # Memoization table\n    memo = {}\n\n    def dp(remaining_districts, used_tiles, current_placements):\n        if not remaining_districts:\n            return current_placements, calculate_total_adjacency(current_placements)\n\n        state = (tuple(sorted(used_tiles)), tuple(remaining_districts))\n        if state in memo:\n            return memo[state]\n\n        best_placement = current_placements\n        best_score = calculate_total_adjacency(current_placements)\n\n        district = remaining_districts[0]\n        rest = remaining_districts[1:]\n\n        for tile in valid_tiles:\n            if tile not in used_tiles:\n                new_placements = current_placements.copy()\n                new_placements[tile] = district\n\n                result_placements, result_score = dp(\n                    rest,\n                    used_tiles | {tile},\n                    new_placements\n                )\n\n                if result_score > best_score:\n                    best_score = result_score\n                    best_placement = result_placements\n\n        memo[state] = (best_placement, best_score)\n        return best_placement, best_score\n\n    return dp(districts, set(), {})\n```\n\n## Strategy Selection\n\n### When to Use Each Approach\n\n**Exhaustive Search:**\n- Map size < 10x10\n- Districts ≤ 3\n- Guarantees optimal solution\n\n**Greedy:**\n- Quick approximation needed\n- Large search spaces\n- Usually 70-90% of optimal\n\n**Hill Climbing:**\n- Medium-sized problems\n- When greedy isn't good enough\n- Can combine with greedy for initial solution\n\n**Heuristic-Guided:**\n- Large problems\n- Domain knowledge available\n- Pre-filter tiles by natural features\n\n## Practical Implementation\n\n```python\nclass DistrictOptimizer:\n    def __init__(self, map_data):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n\n    def optimize(self, city_center, population, strategy='auto'):\n        \"\"\"Main optimization entry point\"\"\"\n        max_districts = self.get_max_districts(population)\n\n        if strategy == 'auto':\n            # Auto-select based on problem size\n            if max_districts <= 3:\n                strategy = 'exhaustive'\n            elif max_districts <= 5:\n                strategy = 'hill_climbing'\n            else:\n                strategy = 'greedy'\n\n        if strategy == 'exhaustive':\n            return self.exhaustive_search(city_center, max_districts)\n        elif strategy == 'greedy':\n            return self.greedy_placement(city_center, max_districts)\n        elif strategy == 'hill_climbing':\n            # Start with greedy, improve with hill climbing\n            initial = self.greedy_placement(city_center, max_districts)\n            return self.hill_climbing(city_center, initial)\n\n    def get_max_districts(self, population):\n        \"\"\"Calculate max districts based on population\"\"\"\n        if population < 4:\n            return 0\n        return (population - 1) // 3\n```\n\n## District Selection Heuristics\n\n### Prioritizing Districts\n\n```python\ndef select_districts(map_data, city_center, max_districts):\n    \"\"\"\n    Intelligently choose which districts to build\n    \"\"\"\n    # Analyze map features near city\n    nearby_features = analyze_nearby_features(map_data, city_center, radius=3)\n\n    district_scores = {}\n\n    # Score each district type based on available bonuses\n    if nearby_features['mountains'] >= 2:\n        district_scores['CAMPUS'] = nearby_features['mountains'] * 2\n        district_scores['HOLY_SITE'] = nearby_features['mountains'] * 2\n\n    if nearby_features['rainforest'] >= 2:\n        district_scores['CAMPUS'] = district_scores.get('CAMPUS', 0) + nearby_features['rainforest']\n\n    if nearby_features['river_tiles'] >= 1:\n        district_scores['COMMERCIAL_HUB'] = nearby_features['river_tiles'] * 3\n\n    # Industrial zones benefit from clustering\n    district_scores['INDUSTRIAL_ZONE'] = max_districts * 0.5  # More districts = better IZ\n\n    # Sort and select top districts\n    sorted_districts = sorted(district_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n    return [d[0] for d in sorted_districts[:max_districts]]\n```\n\n## Notes\n- Problem complexity grows as O(n^d) where n=tiles, d=districts\n- Adjacency calculations must account for order (district A might boost district B)\n- City center location is often the most important decision\n- Some district combinations synergize better than Given constraints (map size, number of cities, population limits), find the placement that maximizes total adjacency bonuses.\n\n## Problem Formulation\n\n### Decision Variables\n- City center location(s): (x, y) coordinates\n- District types: Which districts to build\n- District placements: (x, y) coordinates for each district\n\n### Constraints\n1. **Population limits**: Number of districts ≤ f(population)\n   - Pop 1-3: 0 districts\n   - Pop 4-6: 1 district\n   - Pop 7-9: 2 districts\n   - Pop 10-12: 3 districts\n\n2. **Placement validity**:\n   - Within 3 tiles of city center\n   - Valid terrain (not mountains, not ocean except harbor)\n   - No overlapping districts\n\n3. **Map boundaries**: All placements within map dimensions\n\n### Objective\nMaximize: Σ(adjacency_bonus(district_i))\n\n## Optimization Approaches\n\n### 1. Exhaustive Search (Small Spaces)\nFor small problems, enumerate all valid combinations.\n\n```python\ndef exhaustive_search(map_data, city_center, max_districts, district_types):\n    \"\"\"\n    Try all valid combinations of district placements\n    \"\"\"\n    workable_tiles = get_tiles_in_range(city_center, 3)\n    valid_tiles = [t for t in workable_tiles if is_valid_for_district(t)]\n\n    best_solution = None\n    best_score = -1\n\n    # For each combination of tiles\n    from itertools import combinations\n    for tiles in combinations(valid_tiles, max_districts):\n        # For each permutation of district types\n        from itertools import permutations\n        for district_assignment in permutations(district_types, max_districts):\n            placements = dict(zip(tiles, district_assignment))\n            score = calculate_total_adjacency(placements)\n\n            if score > best_score:\n                best_score = score\n                best_solution = placements\n\n    return best_solution, best_score\n```\n\n### 2. Greedy Algorithm\nBuild districts one at a time, always choosing the placement with highest marginal benefit.\n\n```python\ndef greedy_placement(map_data, city_center, districts_to_build):\n    \"\"\"\n    Greedy algorithm: place each district where it gets best bonus\n    \"\"\"\n    placements = {}\n    workable_tiles = get_tiles_in_range(city_center, 3)\n\n    for district_type in districts_to_build:\n        best_tile = None\n        best_bonus = -1\n\n        for tile in workable_tiles:\n            if is_valid_placement(tile, placements):\n                # Calculate bonus if we place district here\n                bonus = calculate_district_adjacency(\n                    district_type, tile, placements, map_data\n                )\n\n                if bonus > best_bonus:\n                    best_bonus = bonus\n                    best_tile = tile\n\n        if best_tile:\n            placements[best_tile] = district_type\n\n    return placements\n```\n\n### 3. Hill Climbing / Local Search\nStart with random/greedy solution, iteratively improve by swapping.\n\n```python\ndef hill_climbing(map_data, city_center, initial_placements, max_iterations=1000):\n    \"\"\"\n    Hill climbing: start with solution, iteratively swap to improve\n    \"\"\"\n    current = initial_placements.copy()\n    current_score = calculate_total_adjacency(current)\n\n    workable_tiles = get_tiles_in_range(city_center, 3)\n\n    for _ in range(max_iterations):\n        improved = False\n\n        # Try swapping each district to a different tile\n        for tile, district in list(current.items()):\n            for new_tile in workable_tiles:\n                if new_tile not in current and is_valid_placement(new_tile):\n                    # Try the swap\n                    test = current.copy()\n                    del test[tile]\n                    test[new_tile] = district\n\n                    test_score = calculate_total_adjacency(test)\n                    if test_score > current_score:\n                        current = test\n                        current_score = test_score\n                        improved = True\n                        break\n\n            if improved:\n                break\n\n        if not improved:\n            break  # Local optimum found\n\n    return current, current_score\n```\n\n### 4. Dynamic Programming (Limited Cases)\nFor certain structured problems, DP can find optimal solutions.\n\n```python\ndef dp_optimization(map_data, city_center, districts, max_districts):\n    \"\"\"\n    Dynamic programming approach for district placement\n    State: (tiles_used, districts_placed)\n    \"\"\"\n    workable_tiles = get_tiles_in_range(city_center, 3)\n    valid_tiles = [t for t in workable_tiles if is_valid_for_district(t)]\n\n    # Memoization table\n    memo = {}\n\n    def dp(remaining_districts, used_tiles, current_placements):\n        if not remaining_districts:\n            return current_placements, calculate_total_adjacency(current_placements)\n\n        state = (tuple(sorted(used_tiles)), tuple(remaining_districts))\n        if state in memo:\n            return memo[state]\n\n        best_placement = current_placements\n        best_score = calculate_total_adjacency(current_placements)\n\n        district = remaining_districts[0]\n        rest = remaining_districts[1:]\n\n        for tile in valid_tiles:\n            if tile not in used_tiles:\n                new_placements = current_placements.copy()\n                new_placements[tile] = district\n\n                result_placements, result_score = dp(\n                    rest,\n                    used_tiles | {tile},\n                    new_placements\n                )\n\n                if result_score > best_score:\n                    best_score = result_score\n                    best_placement = result_placements\n\n        memo[state] = (best_placement, best_score)\n        return best_placement, best_score\n\n    return dp(districts, set(), {})\n```\n\n## Strategy Selection\n\n### When to Use Each Approach\n\n**Exhaustive Search:**\n- Map size < 10x10\n- Districts ≤ 3\n- Guarantees optimal solution\n\n**Greedy:**\n- Quick approximation needed\n- Large search spaces\n- Usually 70-90% of optimal\n\n**Hill Climbing:**\n- Medium-sized problems\n- When greedy isn't good enough\n- Can combine with greedy for initial solution\n\n**Heuristic-Guided:**\n- Large problems\n- Domain knowledge available\n- Pre-filter tiles by natural features\n\n## Practical Implementation\n\n```python\nclass DistrictOptimizer:\n    def __init__(self, map_data):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n\n    def optimize(self, city_center, population, strategy='auto'):\n        \"\"\"Main optimization entry point\"\"\"\n        max_districts = self.get_max_districts(population)\n\n        if strategy == 'auto':\n            # Auto-select based on problem size\n            if max_districts <= 3:\n                strategy = 'exhaustive'\n            elif max_districts <= 5:\n                strategy = 'hill_climbing'\n            else:\n                strategy = 'greedy'\n\n        if strategy == 'exhaustive':\n            return self.exhaustive_search(city_center, max_districts)\n        elif strategy == 'greedy':\n            return self.greedy_placement(city_center, max_districts)\n        elif strategy == 'hill_climbing':\n            # Start with greedy, improve with hill climbing\n            initial = self.greedy_placement(city_center, max_districts)\n            return self.hill_climbing(city_center, initial)\n\n    def get_max_districts(self, population):\n        \"\"\"Calculate max districts based on population\"\"\"\n        if population < 4:\n            return 0\n        return (population - 1) // 3\n```\n\n## District Selection Heuristics\n\n### Prioritizing Districts\n\n```python\ndef select_districts(map_data, city_center, max_districts):\n    \"\"\"\n    Intelligently choose which districts to build\n    \"\"\"\n    # Analyze map features near city\n    nearby_features = analyze_nearby_features(map_data, city_center, radius=3)\n\n    district_scores = {}\n\n    # Score each district type based on available bonuses\n    if nearby_features['mountains'] >= 2:\n        district_scores['CAMPUS'] = nearby_features['mountains'] * 2\n        district_scores['HOLY_SITE'] = nearby_features['mountains'] * 2\n\n    if nearby_features['rainforest'] >= 2:\n        district_scores['CAMPUS'] = district_scores.get('CAMPUS', 0) + nearby_features['rainforest']\n\n    if nearby_features['river_tiles'] >= 1:\n        district_scores['COMMERCIAL_HUB'] = nearby_features['river_tiles'] * 3\n\n    # Industrial zones benefit from clustering\n    district_scores['INDUSTRIAL_ZONE'] = max_districts * 0.5  # More districts = better IZ\n\n    # Sort and select top districts\n    sorted_districts = sorted(district_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n    return [d[0] for d in sorted_districts[:max_districts]]\n```\n\n## Notes\n- Problem complexity grows as O(n^d) where n=tiles, d=districts\n- Adjacency calculations must account for order (district A might boost district        \"\"\"Get all 6 hexagonal neighbors for even-q vertical layout\"\"\"\n        if y % 2 == 0:  # Even column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, -1), (1, 0), (0, 1), (-1, 0), (-1, -1)]\n        else:  # Odd column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, 0), (1, 1), (0, 1), (-1, 1), (-1, 0)]\n\n        neighbors = []\n        for dx, dy in offsets:\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            if self.is_valid(nx, ny):\n                neighbors.append((nx, ny))\n        return neighbors\n\n    def get_tiles_in_range(self, center_x, center_y, radius):\n        \"\"\"Get all tiles within radius of center using BFS\"\"\"\n        if not self.is_valid(center_x, center_y):\n            return []\n\n        tiles = set()\n        tiles.add((center_x, center_y))\n        current_ring = {(center_x, center_y)}\n\n        for _ in range(radius):\n            next_ring = set()\n            for tile_x, tile_y in current_ring:\n                for neighbor in self.get_neighbors(tile_x, tile_y):\n                    if neighbor not in tiles:\n                        next_ring.add(neighbor)\n                        tiles.add(neighbor)\n            current_ring = next_ring\n\n Even column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, -1), (1, 0), (0, 1), (-1, 0), (-1, -1)]\n        else:  # Odd column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, 0), (1, 1), (0, 1), (-1, 1), (-1, 0)]\n\n        neighbors = []\n        for dx, dy in offsets:\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            if self.is_valid(nx, ny):\n                neighbors.append((nx, ny))\n        return neighbors\n\n    def get_tiles_in_range(self, center_x, center_y, radius):\n        \"\"\"Get all tiles within radius of center using BFS\"\"\"\n        if not self.is_valid(center_x, center_y):\n            return []\n\n        tiles = set()\n        tiles.add((center_x, center_y))\n        current_ring = {(center_x, center_y)}\n\n        for _ in range(radius):\n            next_ring = set()\n            for tile_x, tile_y in current_ring:\n                for neighbor in self.get_neighbors(tile_x, tile_y):\n                    if neighbor not in tiles:\n                        next_ring.add(neighbor)\n                        tiles.add(neighbor)\n            current_ring = next_ring\n\n        return list(tiles)\n\n\nclass Civ6MapParser:\n    \"\"\"Parser for Civ6Map SQLite database files\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, filepath):\n        self.filepath = filepath\n        self.width = 0\n        self.height = 0\n        self.terrain = {}\n        self.features = {}\n        self.resources = {}\n        self.plots = {}\n\n    def parse(self):\n        \"\"\"Parse the SQLite database\"\"\"\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.filepath)\n        cursor = conn.cursor()\n\n        # Get map dimensions from Map table\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT Width, Height FROM Map LIMIT 1\")\n        result = cursor.fetchone()\n        if result:\n            self.width = int(result[0])\n            self.height = int(result[1])\n\n        # Get plot data\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, TerrainType FROM Plots\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, terrain = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.terrain[(x, y)] = terrain if terrain else 'UNKNOWN'\n            self.plots[plot_id] = (x, y)\n\n        # Get features\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, FeatureType FROM PlotFeatures WHERE FeatureType IS NOT NULL\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, feature = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.features[(x, y)] = feature\n\n        # Get resources\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, ResourceType FROM PlotResources WHERE ResourceType IS NOT NULL\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, resource = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.resources[(x, y)] = resource\n\n        conn.close()\n\n    def get_tile_info(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Get all information about a specific tile\"\"\"\n        terrain = self.terrain.get((x, y), 'UNKNOWN')\n        # Check if terrain is a mountain type\n        is_mountain = 'MOUNTAIN' in terrain\n\n        return {\n            'terrain': terrain,\n            'feature': self.features.get((x, y), None),\n            'resource': self.resources.get((x, y), None),\n            'is_mountain': is_mountain\n        }\n\n    def is_valid_tile(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Check if coordinates are within map bounds\"\"\"\n        return 0 <= x < self.width and 0 <= y < self.height\n\n\nclass AdjacencyCalculator:\n    \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonuses for Civ6 districts\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, map_data, hex_grid):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n        self.hex_grid = hex_grid\n\n    def calculate_district_adjacency(self, district_type, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonus for a district at (x, y)\"\"\"\n        if district_type == 'CAMPUS':\n            return self._calc_campus(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'HOLY_SITE':\n            return self._calc_holy_site(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'THEATER_SQUARE':\n            return self._calc_theater_square(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'COMMERCIAL_HUB':\n            return self._calc_commercial_hub(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'HARBOR':\n            return self._calc_harbor(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE':\n            return self._calc_industrial_zone(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n            return self._calc_entertainment_complex(x, y, placements)\n        else:\n            return 0\n\n    def _calc_campus(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Campus: +1 per mountain, +1 per rainforest, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Mountains: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Rainforest/Jungle: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Reef: +1 per 2\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_REEF':\n                district_count += 0.5\n\n            # Count other districts (not City Center)\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_holy_site(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Holy Site: +1 per mountain, +1 per 2 woods, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        woods_count = 0\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Mountains: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Woods: +1 per 2\n            if tile.get('feature') in ['FEATURE_FOREST', 'FEATURE_WOODS']:\n                woods_count += 1\n\n            # Count other districts\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += woods_count // 2\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_theater_square(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Theater Square: +1 per 2 districts, +2 per entertainment complex\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n                    bonus += 2\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_commercial_hub(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Commercial Hub: +1 per 2 districts, +2 per harbor on river, +1 per harbor\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'HARBOR':\n                    bonus += 1\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_harbor(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Harbor: +1 per sea resource, +2 per city center, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Sea resources\n            resource = tile.get('resource')\n            terrain = tile.get('terrain')\n            if resource and terrain in ['TERRAIN_COAST', 'TERRAIN_OCEAN']:\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # City center: +2\n            if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] == 'CITY_CENTER':\n                bonus += 2\n\n            # Other districts\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_industrial_zone(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Industrial Zone: +1 per mine/quarry, +2 per aqueduct/dam, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Strategic resources count as mines\n            resource = tile.get('resource')\n            if resource and 'STRATEGIC' in resource:\n                district_count += 0.5\n\n            # Aqueduct bonus\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'AQUEDUCT':\n                    bonus += 2\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_entertainment_complex(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Entertainment Complex: +1 per adjacent entertainment/water park\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district in ['ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX', 'WATER_PARK']:\n                    bonus += 1\n\n        return int(bonus)\n\n\nclass DistrictOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Optimize district placement for maximum adjacency\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, map_data, hex_grid):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n        self.hex_grid = hex_grid\n        self.adjacency_calc = AdjacencyCalculator(map_data, hex_grid)\n\n    def get_max_districts(self, population):\n        \"\"\"Calculate max districts based on population\"\"\"\n        if population < 4:\n            return 0\n        return (population - 1) // 3\n\n    def is_valid_placement(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Check if a tile is valid for district placement\"\"\"\n        if not self.map_data.is_valid_tile(x, y):\n            return False\n\n        # Already occupied\n        if (x, y) in placements:\n            return False\n\n        tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n        terrain = tile.get('terrain', '')\n        feature = tile.get('feature', '')\n\n        # Cannot build on mountains\n        if feature == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n            return False\n\n        # Cannot build on ocean (except harbor)\n        if terrain == 'TERRAIN_OCEAN':\n            return False\n\n        return True\n\n    def select_districts(self, city_center, max_districts):\n        \"\"\"Intelligently choose which districts to build\"\"\"\n        workable = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n\n        # Count nearby features\n        mountains = 0\n        rainforest = 0\n        water = 0\n\n        for x, y in workable:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                mountains += 1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                rainforest += 1\n            if tile.get('terrain') in ['TERRAIN_COAST', 'TERRAIN_OCEAN']:\n                water += 1\n\n        # Score districts\n        district_scores = {}\n\n        # Campus benefits from mountains and rainforest\n        if mountains >= 1 or rainforest >= 1:\n            district_scores['CAMPUS'] = mountains * 2 + rainforest\n\n        # Holy site benefits from mountains\n        if mountains >= 1:\n            district_scores['HOLY_SITE'] = mountains * 2\n\n        # Harbor if near water\n        if water >= 3:\n            district_scores['HARBOR'] = water\n\n        # Industrial zone benefits from clustering\n        district_scores['INDUSTRIAL_ZONE'] = max_districts * 2\n\n        # Commercial hub\n        district_scores['COMMERCIAL_HUB'] = 5\n\n        # Theater square\n        district_scores['THEATER_SQUARE'] = 3\n\n        # Sort and select top districts\n        sorted_districts = sorted(district_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        selected = [d[0] for d in sorted_districts[:max_districts]]\n\n        # Always ensure we have enough districts\n        all_districts = ['CAMPUS', 'HOLY_SITE', 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE', 'COMMERCIAL_HUB',\n                        'THEATER_SQUARE', 'HARBOR', 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX']\n        for d in all_districts:\n            if len(selected) < max_districts and d not in selected:\n                selected.append(d)\n\n        return selected[:max_districts]\n\n    def optimize_greedy(self, city_center, districts_to_build):\n        \"\"\"Greedy optimization: place each district where it gets best bonus\"\"\"\n        placements = {city_center: 'CITY_CENTER'}\n        workable_tiles = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n\n        # Sort districts by potential benefit\n        for district_type in districts_to_build:\n            best_tile = None\n            best_bonus = -1\n\n            for tile in workable_tiles:\n                if self.is_valid_placement(tile[0], tile[1], placements):\n                    bonus = self.adjacency_calc.calculate_district_adjacency(\n                        district_type, tile[0], tile[1], placements\n                    )\n\n                    if bonus > best_bonus:\n                        best_bonus = bonus\n                        best_tile = tile\n\n            if best_tile:\n                placements[best_tile] = district_type\n\n        return placements\n\n    def optimize_exhaustive(self, city_center, districts_to_build):\n        \"\"\"Try all combinations (for small problems)\"\"\"\n        placements_base = {city_center: 'CITY_CENTER'}\n        workable_tiles = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n        valid_tiles = [t for t in workable_tiles\n                      if self.is_valid_placement(t[0], t[1], placements_base)]\n\n        if len(districts_to_build) > len(valid_tiles):\n            return self.optimize_greedy(city_center, districts_to_build)\n\n        best_solution = None\n        best_score = -1\n\n        # Limit search space\n        max_combinations = 10000\n        tried = 0\n\n        for tiles in combinations(valid_tiles, len(districts_to_build)):\n            if tried >= max_combinations:\n                break\n\n            for district_assignment in permutations(districts_to_build):\n                tried += 1\n                if tried >= max_combinations:\n                    break\n\n                placements = placements_base.copy()\n                for tile, district in zip(tiles, district_assignment):\n                    placements[tile] = district\n\n                score = self.calculate_total_adjacency(placements)\n\n                if score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_solution = placements\n\n        return best_solution if best_solution else self.optimize_greedy(city_center, districts_to_build)\n\n    def calculate_total_adjacency(self, placements):\n        \"\"\"Calculate total adjacency for all districts\"\"\"\n        total = 0\n        for (x, y), district in placements.items():\n            if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                bonus = self.adjacency_calc.calculate_district_adjacency(district, x, y, placements)\n                total += bonus\n        return total\n\n    def find_best_city_location(self, max_districts):\n        \"\"\"Find optimal city center location\"\"\"\n        best_location = None\n        best_score = -1\n\n        # Try different city center locations\n        for y in range(self.map_data.height):\n            for x in range(self.map_data.width):\n                if not self.map_data.is_valid_tile(x, y):\n                    continue\n\n                tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n                if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                    continue\n                if tile.get('terrain') == 'TERRAIN_OCEAN':\n                    continue\n\n                # Quick heuristic: count nearby mountains\n                workable = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(x, y, 3)\n                mountain_count = sum(1 for wx, wy in workable\n                                   if self.map_data.get_tile_info(wx, wy).get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS')\n\n                if mountain_count > best_score:\n                    best_score = mountain_count\n                    best_location = (x, y)\n\n        return best_location if best_location else (self.map_data.width // 2, self.map_data.height // 2)\n\n\ndef solve_scenario(scenario_path, output_path):\n    \"\"\"Main solver function\"\"\"\n    # Load scenario\n    with open(scenario_path, 'r') as f:\n        scenario = json.load(f)\n\n    # Parse map\n    map_path = f\"/data/{scenario['map_file']}\"\n    map_data = Civ6MapParser(map_path)\n    map_data.parse()\n\n    print(f\"Map size: {map_data.width}x{map_data.height}\")\n\n    # Create hex grid\n    hex_grid = HexGrid(map_data.width, map_data.height)\n\n    # Create optimizer\n    optimizer = DistrictOptimizer(map_data, hex_grid)\n\n    # Get constraints\n    num_cities = scenario['num_cities']\n    population = scenario['population']\n    max_districts = optimizer.get_max_districts(population)\n\n    print(f\"Population: {population}, Max districts: {max_districts}\")\n\n    # Find best city center location\n    city_center = optimizer.find_best_city_location(max_districts)\n    print(f\"City center: {city_center}\")\n\n    # Select districts to build\n    districts = optimizer.select_districts(city_center, max_districts)\n    print(f\"Districts to build: {districts}\")\n\n    # Optimize placement\n    if max_districts <= 3:\n        placements = optimizer.optimize_exhaustive(city_center, districts)\n    else:\n        placements = optimizer.optimize_greedy(city_center, districts)\n\n    # Calculate adjacency bonuses\n    adjacency_bonuses = {}\n    for (x, y), district in placements.items():\n        if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n            bonus = optimizer.adjacency_calc.calculate_district_adjacency(district, x, y, placements)\n            adjacency_bonuses[district] = bonus\n\n    total_adjacency = sum(adjacency_bonuses.values())\n\n    print(f\"Total adjacency: {total_adjacency}\")\n    print(f\"Bonuses: {adjacency_bonuses}\")\n\n    # Build output\n    district_placements = {}\n    for (x, y), district in placements.items():\n        if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n            district_placements[district] = [x, y]\n\n    output = {\n        \"city_center\": list(city_center),\n        \"placements\": district_placements,\n        \"adjacency_bonuses\": adjacency_bonuses,\n        \"total_adjacency\": total_adjacency\n    }\n\n    # Write output\n    import os\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(output_path), exist_ok=True)\n    with open(output_path, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Solution written to {output_path}\")\n    return output\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    solve_scenario('/data/scenario_3/scenario.json', '/output/scenario_3.json')\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":146,"oldLines":7,"newStart":146,"newLines":7,"lines":["             return 0"," ","     def _calc_campus(self, x, y, placements):","-        \"\"\"Campus: +1 per mountain, +1 per rainforest, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"","+        \"\"\"Campus: +1 per mountain, +1 per rainforest, +1 per geothermal/reef, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"","         bonus = 0","         neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)","         district_count = 0"]},{"oldStart":154,"oldLines":14,"newStart":154,"newLines":18,"lines":["         for nx, ny in neighbors:","             tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)"," ","-            # Even column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, -1), (1, 0), (0, 1), (-1, 0), (-1, -1)]\n        else:  # Odd column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, 0), (1, 1), (0, 1), (-1, 1), (-1, 0)]\n\n        neighbors = []\n        for dx, dy in offsets:\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            if self.is_valid(nx, ny):\n                neighbors.append((nx, ny))\n        return neighbors\n\n    def get_tiles_in_range(self, center_x, center_y, radius):\n        \"\"\"Get all tiles within radius of center using BFS\"\"\"\n        if not self.is_valid(center_x, center_y):\n            return []\n\n        tiles = set()\n        tiles.add((center_x, center_y))\n        current_ring = {(center_x, center_y)}\n\n        for _ in range(radius):\n            next_ring = set()\n            for tile_x, tile_y in current_ring:\n                for neighbor in self.get_neighbors(tile_x, tile_y):\n                    if neighbor not in tiles:\n                        next_ring.add(neighbor)\n                        tiles.add(neighbor)\n            current_ring = next_ring\n\n        return list(tiles)\n\n\nclass Civ6MapParser:\n    \"\"\"Parser for Civ6Map SQLite database files\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, filepath):\n        self.filepath = filepath\n        self.width = 0\n        self.height = 0\n        self.terrain = {}\n        self.features = {}\n        self.resources = {}\n        self.plots = {}\n\n    def parse(self):\n        \"\"\"Parse the SQLite database\"\"\"\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.filepath)\n        cursor = conn.cursor()\n\n        # Get map dimensions from Map table\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT Width, Height FROM Map LIMIT 1\")\n        result = cursor.fetchone()\n        if result:\n            self.width = int(result[0])\n            self.height = int(result[1])\n\n        # Get plot data\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, TerrainType FROM Plots\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, terrain = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.terrain[(x, y)] = terrain if terrain else 'UNKNOWN'\n            self.plots[plot_id] = (x, y)\n\n        # Get features\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, FeatureType FROM PlotFeatures WHERE FeatureType IS NOT NULL\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, feature = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.features[(x, y)] = feature\n\n        # Get resources\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, ResourceType FROM PlotResources WHERE ResourceType IS NOT NULL\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, resource = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.resources[(x, y)] = resource\n\n        conn.close()\n\n    def get_tile_info(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Get all information about a specific tile\"\"\"\n        terrain = self.terrain.get((x, y), 'UNKNOWN')\n        # Check if terrain is a mountain type\n        is_mountain = 'MOUNTAIN' in terrain\n\n        return {\n            'terrain': terrain,\n            'feature': self.features.get((x, y), None),\n            'resource': self.resources.get((x, y), None),\n            'is_mountain': is_mountain\n        }\n\n    def is_valid_tile(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Check if coordinates are within map bounds\"\"\"\n        return 0 <= x < self.width and 0 <= y < self.height\n\n\nclass AdjacencyCalculator:\n    \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonuses for Civ6 districts\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, map_data, hex_grid):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n        self.hex_grid = hex_grid\n\n    def calculate_district_adjacency(self, district_type, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonus for a district at (x, y)\"\"\"\n        if district_type == 'CAMPUS':\n            return self._calc_campus(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'HOLY_SITE':\n            return self._calc_holy_site(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'THEATER_SQUARE':\n            return self._calc_theater_square(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'COMMERCIAL_HUB':\n            return self._calc_commercial_hub(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'HARBOR':\n            return self._calc_harbor(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE':\n            return self._calc_industrial_zone(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n            return self._calc_entertainment_complex(x, y, placements)\n        else:\n            return 0\n\n    def _calc_campus(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Campus: +1 per mountain, +1 per rainforest, +1 per geothermal/reef, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Mountains: +1 (check both terrain and feature)\n            if tile.get('is_mountain') or tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Rainforest/Jungle: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Geothermal Fissure: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_GEOTHERMAL_FISSURE':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Reef: +1 per 2\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_REEF':\n                district_count += 0.5\n\n            # Count other districts (not City Center)\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_holy_site(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Holy Site: +1 per mountain, +1 per 2 woods, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        woods_count = 0\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Mountains: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Woods: +1 per 2\n            if tile.get('feature') in ['FEATURE_FOREST', 'FEATURE_WOODS']:\n                woods_count += 1\n\n            # Count other districts\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += woods_count // 2\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_theater_square(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Theater Square: +1 per 2 districts, +2 per entertainment complex\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n                    bonus += 2\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_commercial_hub(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Commercial Hub: +1 per 2 districts, +2 per harbor on river, +1 per harbor\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'HARBOR':\n                    bonus += 1\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_harbor(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Harbor: +1 per sea resource, +2 per city center, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Sea resources\n            resource = tile.get('resource')\n            terrain = tile.get('terrain')\n            if resource and terrain in ['TERRAIN_COAST', 'TERRAIN_OCEAN']:\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # City center: +2\n            if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] == 'CITY_CENTER':\n                bonus += 2\n\n            # Other districts\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_industrial_zone(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Industrial Zone: +1 per mine/quarry, +2 per aqueduct/dam, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Strategic resources count as mines\n            resource = tile.get('resource')\n            if resource and 'STRATEGIC' in resource:\n                district_count += 0.5\n\n            # Aqueduct bonus\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'AQUEDUCT':\n                    bonus += 2\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_entertainment_complex(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Entertainment Complex: +1 per adjacent entertainment/water park\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district in ['ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX', 'WATER_PARK']:\n                    bonus += 1\n\n        return int(bonus)\n\n\nclass DistrictOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Optimize district placement for maximum adjacency\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, map_data, hex_grid):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n        self.hex_grid = hex_grid\n        self.adjacency_calc = AdjacencyCalculator(map_data, hex_grid)\n\n    def get_max_districts(self, population):\n        \"\"\"Calculate max districts based on population\"\"\"\n        if population < 4:\n            return 0\n        return (population - 1) // 3\n\n    def is_valid_placement(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Check if a tile is valid for district placement\"\"\"\n        if not self.map_data.is_valid_tile(x, y):\n            return False\n\n        # Already occupied\n        if (x, y) in placements:\n            return False\n\n        tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n        terrain = tile.get('terrain', '')\n        feature = tile.get('feature', '')\n\n        # Cannot build on mountains\n        if feature == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n            return False\n\n        # Cannot build on ocean (except harbor)\n        if terrain == 'TERRAIN_OCEAN':\n            return False\n\n        return True\n\n    def select_districts(self, city_center, max_districts):\n        \"\"\"Intelligently choose which districts to build\"\"\"\n        workable = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n\n        # Count nearby features\n        mountains = 0\n        rainforest = 0\n        water = 0\n\n        for x, y in workable:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                mountains += 1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                rainforest += 1\n            if tile.get('terrain') in ['TERRAIN_COAST', 'TERRAIN_OCEAN']:\n                water += 1\n\n        # Score districts\n        district_scores = {}\n\n        # Campus benefits from mountains and rainforest\n        if mountains >= 1 or rainforest >= 1:\n            district_scores['CAMPUS'] = mountains * 2 + rainforest\n\n        # Holy site benefits from mountains\n        if mountains >= 1:\n            district_scores['HOLY_SITE'] = mountains * 2\n\n        # Harbor if near water\n        if water >= 3:\n            district_scores['HARBOR'] = water\n\n        # Industrial zone benefits from clustering\n        district_scores['INDUSTRIAL_ZONE'] = max_districts * 2\n\n        # Commercial hub\n        district_scores['COMMERCIAL_HUB'] = 5\n\n        # Theater square\n        district_scores['THEATER_SQUARE'] = 3\n\n        # Sort and select top districts\n        sorted_districts = sorted(district_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        selected = [d[0] for d in sorted_districts[:max_districts]]\n\n        # Always ensure we have enough districts\n        all_districts = ['CAMPUS', 'HOLY_SITE', 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE', 'COMMERCIAL_HUB',\n                        'THEATER_SQUARE', 'HARBOR', 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX']\n        for d in all_districts:\n            if len(selected) < max_districts and d not in selected:\n                selected.append(d)\n\n        return selected[:max_districts]\n\n    def optimize_greedy(self, city_center, districts_to_build):\n        \"\"\"Greedy optimization: place each district where it gets best bonus\"\"\"\n        placements = {city_center: 'CITY_CENTER'}\n        workable_tiles = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n\n        # Sort districts by potential benefit\n        for district_type in districts_to_build:\n            best_tile = None\n            best_bonus = -1\n\n            for tile in workable_tiles:\n                if self.is_valid_placement(tile[0], tile[1], placements):\n                    bonus = self.adjacency_calc.calculate_district_adjacency(\n                        district_type, tile[0], tile[1], placements\n                    )\n\n                    if bonus > best_bonus:\n                        best_bonus = bonus\n                        best_tile = tile\n\n            if best_tile:\n                placements[best_tile] = district_type\n\n        return placements\n\n    def optimize_exhaustive(self, city_center, districts_to_build):\n        \"\"\"Try all combinations (for small problems)\"\"\"\n        placements_base = {city_center: 'CITY_CENTER'}\n        workable_tiles = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n        valid_tiles = [t for t in workable_tiles\n                      if self.is_valid_placement(t[0], t[1], placements_base)]\n\n        if len(districts_to_build) > len(valid_tiles):\n            return self.optimize_greedy(city_center, districts_to_build)\n\n        best_solution = None\n        best_score = -1\n\n        # Limit search space\n        max_combinations = 10000\n        tried = 0\n\n        for tiles in combinations(valid_tiles, len(districts_to_build)):\n            if tried >= max_combinations:\n                break\n\n            for district_assignment in permutations(districts_to_build):\n                tried += 1\n                if tried >= max_combinations:\n                    break\n\n                placements = placements_base.copy()\n                for tile, district in zip(tiles, district_assignment):\n                    placements[tile] = district\n\n                score = self.calculate_total_adjacency(placements)\n\n                if score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_solution = placements\n\n        return best_solution if best_solution else self.optimize_greedy(city_center, districts_to_build)\n\n    def calculate_total_adjacency(self, placements):\n        \"\"\"Calculate total adjacency for all districts\"\"\"\n        total = 0\n        for (x, y), district in placements.items():\n            if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                bonus = self.adjacency_calc.calculate_district_adjacency(district, x, y, placements)\n                total += bonus\n        return total\n\n    def find_best_city_location(self, max_districts):\n        \"\"\"Find optimal city center location\"\"\"\n        best_location = None\n        best_score = -1\n\n        # Try different city center locations\n        for y in range(self.map_data.height):\n            for x in range(self.map_data.width):\n                if not self.map_data.is_valid_tile(x, y):\n                    continue\n\n                tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n                if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                    continue\n                if tile.get('terrain') == 'TERRAIN_OCEAN':\n                    continue\n\n                # Already occupied\n        if (x, y) in placements:\n            return False\n\n        tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n        terrain = tile.get('terrain', '')\n        feature = tile.get('feature', '')\n\n        #
