Even column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, -1), (1, 0), (0, 1), (-1, 0), (-1, -1)]\n        else:  # Odd column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, 0), (1, 1), (0, 1), (-1, 1), (-1, 0)]\n\n        neighbors = []\n        for dx, dy in offsets:\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            if self.is_valid(nx, ny):\n                neighbors.append((nx, ny))\n        return neighbors\n\n    def get_tiles_in_range(self, center_x, center_y, radius):\n        \"\"\"Get all tiles within radius of center using BFS\"\"\"\n        if not self.is_valid(center_x, center_y):\n            return []\n\n        tiles = set()\n        tiles.add((center_x, center_y))\n        current_ring = {(center_x, center_y)}\n\n        for _ in range(radius):\n            next_ring = set()\n            for tile_x, tile_y in current_ring:\n                for neighbor in self.get_neighbors(tile_x, tile_y):\n                    if neighbor not in tiles:\n                        next_ring.add(neighbor)\n                        tiles.add(neighbor)\n            current_ring = next_ring\n\n        return list(tiles)\n\n\nclass Civ6MapParser:\n    \"\"\"Parser for Civ6Map SQLite database files\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, filepath):\n        self.filepath = filepath\n        self.width = 0\n        self.height = 0\n        self.terrain = {}\n        self.features = {}\n        self.resources = {}\n        self.plots = {}\n\n    def parse(self):\n        \"\"\"Parse the SQLite database\"\"\"\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.filepath)\n        cursor = conn.cursor()\n\n        # Get map dimensions from Map table\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT Width, Height FROM Map LIMIT 1\")\n        result = cursor.fetchone()\n        if result:\n            self.width = int(result[0])\n            self.height = int(result[1])\n\n        # Get plot data\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, TerrainType FROM Plots\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, terrain = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.terrain[(x, y)] = terrain if terrain else 'UNKNOWN'\n            self.plots[plot_id] = (x, y)\n\n        # Get features\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, FeatureType FROM PlotFeatures WHERE FeatureType IS NOT NULL\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, feature = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.features[(x, y)] = feature\n\n        # Get resources\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, ResourceType FROM PlotResources WHERE ResourceType IS NOT NULL\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, resource = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.resources[(x, y)] = resource\n\n        conn.close()\n\n    def get_tile_info(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Get all information about a specific tile\"\"\"\n        terrain = self.terrain.get((x, y), 'UNKNOWN')\n        # Check if terrain is a mountain type\n        is_mountain = 'MOUNTAIN' in terrain\n\n        return {\n            'terrain': terrain,\n            'feature': self.features.get((x, y), None),\n            'resource': self.resources.get((x, y), None),\n            'is_mountain': is_mountain\n        }\n\n    def is_valid_tile(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Check if coordinates are within map bounds\"\"\"\n        return 0 <= x < self.width and 0 <= y < self.height\n\n\nclass AdjacencyCalculator:\n    \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonuses for Civ6 districts\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, map_data, hex_grid):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n        self.hex_grid = hex_grid\n\n    def calculate_district_adjacency(self, district_type, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonus for a district at (x, y)\"\"\"\n        if district_type == 'CAMPUS':\n            return self._calc_campus(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'HOLY_SITE':\n            return self._calc_holy_site(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'THEATER_SQUARE':\n            return self._calc_theater_square(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'COMMERCIAL_HUB':\n            return self._calc_commercial_hub(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'HARBOR':\n            return self._calc_harbor(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE':\n            return self._calc_industrial_zone(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n            return self._calc_entertainment_complex(x, y, placements)\n        else:\n            return 0\n\n    def _calc_campus(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Campus: +1 per mountain, +1 per rainforest, +1 per geothermal/reef, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Mountains: +1 (check both terrain and feature)\n            if tile.get('is_mountain') or tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Rainforest/Jungle: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Geothermal Fissure: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_GEOTHERMAL_FISSURE':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Reef: +1 per 2\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_REEF':\n                district_count += 0.5\n\n            # Count other districts (not City Center)\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_holy_site(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Holy Site: +1 per mountain, +1 per 2 woods, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        woods_count = 0\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Mountains: +1 (check both terrain and feature)\n            if tile.get('is_mountain') or tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Woods: +1 per 2\n            if tile.get('feature') in ['FEATURE_FOREST', 'FEATURE_WOODS']:\n                woods_count += 1\n\n            # Count other districts\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += woods_count // 2\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_theater_square(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Theater Square: +1 per 2 districts, +2 per entertainment complex\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n                    bonus += 2\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_commercial_hub(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Commercial Hub: +1 per 2 districts, +2 per harbor on river, +1 per harbor\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'HARBOR':\n                    bonus += 1\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_harbor(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Harbor: +1 per sea resource, +2 per city center, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Sea resources\n            resource = tile.get('resource')\n            terrain = tile.get('terrain')\n            if resource and terrain in ['TERRAIN_COAST', 'TERRAIN_OCEAN']:\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # City center: +2\n            if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] == 'CITY_CENTER':\n                bonus += 2\n\n            # Other districts\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_industrial_zone(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Industrial Zone: +1 per mine/quarry, +2 per aqueduct/dam, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Strategic resources count as mines\n            resource = tile.get('resource')\n            if resource and 'STRATEGIC' in resource:\n                district_count += 0.5\n\n            # Aqueduct bonus\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'AQUEDUCT':\n                    bonus += 2\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_entertainment_complex(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Entertainment Complex: +1 per adjacent entertainment/water park\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district in ['ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX', 'WATER_PARK']:\n                    bonus += 1\n\n        return int(bonus)\n\n\nclass DistrictOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Optimize district placement for maximum adjacency\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, map_data, hex_grid):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n        self.hex_grid = hex_grid\n        self.adjacency_calc = AdjacencyCalculator(map_data, hex_grid)\n\n    def get_max_districts(self, population):\n        \"\"\"Calculate max districts based on population\"\"\"\n        if population < 4:\n            return 0\n        return (population - 1) // 3\n\n    def is_valid_placement(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Check if a tile is valid for district placement\"\"\"\n        if not self.map_data.is_valid_tile(x, y):\n            return False\n\n        # Already occupied\n        if (x, y) in placements:\n            return False\n\n        tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n        terrain = tile.get('terrain', '')\n        feature = tile.get('feature', '')\n\n        # Cannot build on mountains\n        if feature == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n            return False\n\n        # Cannot build on ocean (except harbor)\n        if terrain == 'TERRAIN_OCEAN':\n            return False\n\n        return True\n\n    def select_districts(self, city_center, max_districts):\n        \"\"\"Intelligently choose which districts to build\"\"\"\n        workable = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n\n        # Count nearby features\n        mountains = 0\n        rainforest = 0\n        water = 0\n\n        for x, y in workable:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                mountains += 1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                rainforest += 1\n            if tile.get('terrain') in ['TERRAIN_COAST', 'TERRAIN_OCEAN']:\n                water += 1\n\n        # Score districts\n        district_scores = {}\n\n        # Campus benefits from mountains and rainforest\n        if mountains >= 1 or rainforest >= 1:\n            district_scores['CAMPUS'] = mountains * 2 + rainforest\n\n        # Holy site benefits from mountains\n        if mountains >= 1:\n            district_scores['HOLY_SITE'] = mountains * 2\n\n        # Harbor if near water\n        if water >= 3:\n            district_scores['HARBOR'] = water\n\n        # Industrial zone benefits from clustering\n        district_scores['INDUSTRIAL_ZONE'] = max_districts * 2\n\n        # Commercial hub\n        district_scores['COMMERCIAL_HUB'] = 5\n\n        # Theater square\n        district_scores['THEATER_SQUARE'] = 3\n\n        # Sort and select top districts\n        sorted_districts = sorted(district_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        selected = [d[0] for d in sorted_districts[:max_districts]]\n\n        # Even column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, -1), (1, 0), (0, 1), (-1, 0), (-1, -1)]\n        else:  # Odd column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, 0), (1, 1), (0, 1), (-1, 1), (-1, 0)]\n\n        neighbors = []\n        for dx, dy in offsets:\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            if self.is_valid(nx, ny):\n                neighbors.append((nx, ny))\n        return neighbors\n\n    def get_tiles_in_range(self, center_x, center_y, radius):\n        \"\"\"Get all tiles within radius of center using BFS\"\"\"\n        if not self.is_valid(center_x, center_y):\n            return []\n\n        tiles = set()\n        tiles.add((center_x, center_y))\n        current_ring = {(center_x, center_y)}\n\n        for _ in range(radius):\n            next_ring = set()\n            for tile_x, tile_y in current_ring:\n                for neighbor in self.get_neighbors(tile_x, tile_y):\n                    if neighbor not in tiles:\n                        next_ring.add(neighbor)\n                        tiles.add(neighbor)\n            current_ring = next_ring\n\n        return list(tiles)\n\n\nclass Civ6MapParser:\n    \"\"\"Parser for Civ6Map SQLite database files\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, filepath):\n        self.filepath = filepath\n        self.width = 0\n        self.height = 0\n        self.terrain = {}\n        self.features = {}\n        self.resources = {}\n        self.plots = {}\n\n    def parse(self):\n        \"\"\"Parse the SQLite database\"\"\"\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.filepath)\n        cursor = conn.cursor()\n\n        # Get map dimensions from Map table\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT Width, Height FROM Map LIMIT 1\")\n        result = cursor.fetchone()\n        if result:\n            self.width = int(result[0])\n            self.height = int(result[1])\n\n        # Get plot data\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, TerrainType FROM Plots\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, terrain = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.terrain[(x, y)] = terrain if terrain else 'UNKNOWN'\n            self.plots[plot_id] = (x, y)\n\n        # Get features\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, FeatureType FROM PlotFeatures WHERE FeatureType IS NOT NULL\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, feature = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.features[(x, y)] = feature\n\n        # Get resources\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, ResourceType FROM PlotResources WHERE ResourceType IS NOT NULL\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, resource = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.resources[(x, y)] = resource\n\n        conn.close()\n\n    def get_tile_info(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Get all information about a specific tile\"\"\"\n        terrain = self.terrain.get((x, y), 'UNKNOWN')\n        # Check if terrain is a mountain type\n        is_mountain = 'MOUNTAIN' in terrain\n\n        return {\n            'terrain': terrain,\n            'feature': self.features.get((x, y), None),\n            'resource': self.resources.get((x, y), None),\n            'is_mountain': is_mountain\n        }\n\n    def is_valid_tile(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Check if coordinates are within map bounds\"\"\"\n        return 0 <= x < self.width and 0 <= y < self.height\n\n\nclass AdjacencyCalculator:\n    \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonuses for Civ6 districts\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, map_data, hex_grid):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n        self.hex_grid = hex_grid\n\n    def calculate_district_adjacency(self, district_type, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonus for a district at (x, y)\"\"\"\n        if district_type == 'CAMPUS':\n            return self._calc_campus(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'HOLY_SITE':\n            return self._calc_holy_site(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'THEATER_SQUARE':\n            return self._calc_theater_square(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'COMMERCIAL_HUB':\n            return self._calc_commercial_hub(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'HARBOR':\n            return self._calc_harbor(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE':\n            return self._calc_industrial_zone(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n            return self._calc_entertainment_complex(x, y, placements)\n        else:\n            return 0\n\n    def _calc_campus(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Campus: +1 per mountain, +1 per rainforest, +1 per geothermal/reef, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Mountains: +1 (check both terrain and feature)\n            if tile.get('is_mountain') or tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Rainforest/Jungle: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Geothermal Fissure: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_GEOTHERMAL_FISSURE':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Reef: +1 per 2\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_REEF':\n                district_count += 0.5\n\n            # Count other districts (not City Center)\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_holy_site(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Holy Site: +1 per mountain, +1 per 2 woods, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        woods_count = 0\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Mountains: +1 (check both terrain and feature)\n            if tile.get('is_mountain') or tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Woods: +1 per 2\n            if tile.get('feature') in ['FEATURE_FOREST', 'FEATURE_WOODS']:\n                woods_count += 1\n\n            # Count other districts\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += woods_count // 2\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_theater_square(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Theater Square: +1 per 2 districts, +2 per entertainment complex\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n                    bonus += 2\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_commercial_hub(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Commercial Hub: +1 per 2 districts, +2 per harbor on river, +1 per harbor\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'HARBOR':\n                    bonus += 1\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_harbor(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Harbor: +1 per sea resource, +2 per city center, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Sea resources\n            resource = tile.get('resource')\n            terrain = tile.get('terrain')\n            if resource and terrain in ['TERRAIN_COAST', 'TERRAIN_OCEAN']:\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # City center: +2\n            if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] == 'CITY_CENTER':\n                bonus += 2\n\n            # Other districts\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_industrial_zone(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Industrial Zone: +1 per mine/quarry, +2 per aqueduct/dam, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Strategic resources count as mines\n            resource = tile.get('resource')\n            if resource and 'STRATEGIC' in resource:\n                district_count += 0.5\n\n            # Aqueduct bonus\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'AQUEDUCT':\n                    bonus += 2\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_entertainment_complex(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Entertainment Complex: +1 per adjacent entertainment/water park\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district in ['ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX', 'WATER_PARK']:\n                    bonus += 1\n\n        return int(bonus)\n\n\nclass DistrictOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Optimize district placement for maximum adjacency\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, map_data, hex_grid):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n        self.hex_grid = hex_grid\n        self.adjacency_calc = AdjacencyCalculator(map_data, hex_grid)\n\n    def get_max_districts(self, population):\n        \"\"\"Calculate max districts based on population\"\"\"\n        if population < 4:\n            return 0\n        return (population - 1) // 3\n\n    def is_valid_placement(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Check if a tile is valid for district placement\"\"\"\n        if not self.map_data.is_valid_tile(x, y):\n            return False\n\n        # Already occupied\n        if (x, y) in placements:\n            return False\n\n        tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n        terrain = tile.get('terrain', '')\n        feature = tile.get('feature', '')\n\n        # Cannot build on mountains (check terrain type)\n        if tile.get('is_mountain') or feature == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n            return False\n\n        # Cannot build on ocean (except harbor)\n        if terrain == 'TERRAIN_OCEAN':\n            return False\n\n        return True\n\n    def select_districts(self, city_center, max_districts):\n        \"\"\"Intelligently choose which districts to build\"\"\"\n        workable = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n\n        # Count nearby features\n        mountains = 0\n        rainforest = 0\n        water = 0\n\n        for x, y in workable:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                mountains += 1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                rainforest += 1\n            if tile.get('terrain') in ['TERRAIN_COAST', 'TERRAIN_OCEAN']:\n                water += 1\n\n        # Score districts\n        district_scores = {}\n\n        # Campus benefits from mountains and rainforest\n        if mountains >= 1 or rainforest >= 1:\n            district_scores['CAMPUS'] = mountains * 2 + rainforest\n\n        # Holy site benefits from mountains\n        if mountains >= 1:\n            district_scores['HOLY_SITE'] = mountains * 2\n\n        # Harbor if near water\n        if water >= 3:\n            district_scores['HARBOR'] = water\n\n        # Industrial zone benefits from clustering\n        district_scores['INDUSTRIAL_ZONE'] = max_districts * 2\n\n        # Commercial hub\n        district_scores['COMMERCIAL_HUB'] = 5\n\n        # Theater square\n        district_scores['THEATER_SQUARE'] = 3\n\n        # Sort and select top districts\n        sorted_districts = sorted(district_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        selected = [d[0] for d in sorted_districts[:max_districts]]\n\n        # Always ensure we have enough districts\n        all_districts = ['CAMPUS', 'HOLY_SITE', 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE', 'COMMERCIAL_HUB',\n                        'THEATER_SQUARE', 'HARBOR', 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX']\n        for d in all_districts:\n            if len(selected) < max_districts and d not in selected:\n                selected.append(d)\n\n        return selected[:max_districts]\n\n    def optimize_greedy(self, city_center, districts_to_build):\n        \"\"\"Greedy optimization: place each district where it gets best bonus\"\"\"\n        placements = {city_center: 'CITY_CENTER'}\n        workable_tiles = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n\n        # Sort districts by potential benefit\n        for district_type in districts_to_build:\n            best_tile = None\n            best_bonus = -1\n\n            for tile in workable_tiles:\n                if self.is_valid_placement(tile[0], tile[1], placements):\n                    bonus = self.adjacency_calc.calculate_district_adjacency(\n                        district_type, tile[0], tile[1], placements\n                    )\n\n                    if bonus > best_bonus:\n                        best_bonus = bonus\n                        best_tile = tile\n\n            if best_tile:\n                placements[best_tile] = district_type\n\n        return placements\n\n    def optimize_exhaustive(self, city_center, districts_to_build):\n        \"\"\"Try all combinations (for small problems)\"\"\"\n        placements_base = {city_center: 'CITY_CENTER'}\n        workable_tiles = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n        valid_tiles = [t for t in workable_tiles\n                      if self.is_valid_placement(t[0], t[1], placements_base)]\n\n        if len(districts_to_build) > len(valid_tiles):\n            return self.optimize_greedy(city_center, districts_to_build)\n\n        best_solution = None\n        best_score = -1\n\n        # Limit search space\n        max_combinations = 10000\n        tried = 0\n\n        for tiles in combinations(valid_tiles, len(districts_to_build)):\n            if tried >= max_combinations:\n                break\n\n            for district_assignment in permutations(districts_to_build):\n                tried += 1\n                if tried >= max_combinations:\n                    break\n\n                placements = placements_base.copy()\n                for tile, district in zip(tiles, district_assignment):\n                    placements[tile] = district\n\n                score = self.calculate_total_adjacency(placements)\n\n                if score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_solution = placements\n\n        return best_solution if best_solution else self.optimize_greedy(city_center, districts_to_build)\n\n    def calculate_total_adjacency(self, placements):\n        \"\"\"Calculate total adjacency for all districts\"\"\"\n        total = 0\n        for (x, y), district in placements.items():\n            if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                bonus = self.adjacency_calc.calculate_district_adjacency(district, x, y, placements)\n                total += bonus\n        return total\n\n    def find_best_city_location(self, max_districts):\n        \"\"\"Find optimal city center location\"\"\"\n        best_location = None\n        best_score = -1\n\n        # Try different city center locations\n        for y in range(self.map_data.height):\n            for x in range(self.map_data.width):\n                if not self.map_data.is_valid_tile(x, y):\n                    continue\n\n                tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n                if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                    continue\n                if tile.get('terrain') == 'TERRAIN_OCEAN':\n                    continue\n\n                # Quick heuristic: count nearby mountains\n                workable = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(x, y, 3)\n                mountain_count = sum(1 for wx, wy in workable\n                                   if self.map_data.get_tile_info(wx, wy).get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS')\n\n                if mountain_count > best_score:\n                    best_score = mountain_count\n                    best_location = (x, y)\n\n        return best_location if best_location else (self.map_data.width // 2, self.map_data.height // 2)\n\n\ndef solve_scenario(scenario_path, output_path):\n    \"\"\"Main solver function\"\"\"\n    # Find best city center location\n    city_center = optimizer.find_best_city_location(max_districts)\n    print(f\"City center: {city_center}\")\n\n    # Select districts to build\n    districts = optimizer.select_districts(city_center, max_districts)\n    print(f\"Districts to build: {districts}\")\n\n    # Calculate adjacency bonuses\n    adjacency_bonuses = {}\n    for (x, y), district in placements.items():\n        if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n            bonus = optimizer.adjacency_calc.calculate_district_adjacency(district, x, y, placements)\n            adjacency_bonuses[district] = bonus\n\n    total_adjacency = sum(adjacency_bonuses.values())\n\n    print(f\"Total adjacency: {total_adjacency}\")\n    print(f\"Bonuses: {adjacency_bonuses}\")\n\n    # Build output\n    district_placements = {}\n    for (x, y), district in placements.items():\n        if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n            district_placements[district] = [x, y]\n\n    output = {\n        \"city_center\": list(city_center),\n        \"placements\": district_placements,\n        \"adjacency_bonuses\": adjacency_bonuses,\n        \"total_adjacency\": total_adjacency\n    }\n\n    # Try different city center locations\n        for y in range(self.map_data.height):\n            for x in range(self.map_data.width):\n                if not self.map_data.is_valid_tile(x, y):\n                    continue\n\n                tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n                if tile.get('is_mountain') or tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                    continue\n                if tile.get('terrain') == 'TERRAIN_OCEAN':\n                    continue\n\n                # Heuristic: count nearby valuable features\n                workable = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(x, y, 3)\n                score = 0\n                for wx, wy in workable:\n                    wtile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(wx, wy)\n                    if wtile.get('is_mountain'):\n                        score += 5\n                    if wtile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                        score += 3\n                    if wtile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_GEOTHERMAL_FISSURE':\n                        score += 3\n                    if wtile.get('feature') in ['FEATURE_FOREST', 'FEATURE_WOODS']:\n                        score += 1\n\n                if score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_location = (x, y)\n\n        return best_location if best_location else (self.map_data.width // 2, self.map_data.height // 2)","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\nimport json\nimport sqlite3\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom itertools import combinations, permutations\n\nclass HexGrid:\n    \"\"\"Hexagonal grid utilities for even-q vertical layout\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, width, height):\n        self.width = width\n        self.height = height\n\n    def is_valid(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Check if coordinates are within bounds\"\"\"\n        return 0 <= x < self.width and 0 <= y < self.height\n\n    def get_neighbors(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Get all 6 hexagonal neighbors for even-q vertical layout\"\"\"\n        if y % 2 == 0:  # Even column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, -1), (1, 0), (0, 1), (-1, 0), (-1, -1)]\n        else:  # Odd column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, 0), (1, 1), (0, 1), (-1, 1), (-1, 0)]\n\n        neighbors = []\n        for dx, dy in offsets:\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            if self.is_valid(nx, ny):\n                neighbors.append((nx, ny))\n        return neighbors\n\n    def get_tiles_in_range(self, center_x, center_y, radius):\n        \"\"\"Get all tiles within radius of center using BFS\"\"\"\n        if not self.is_valid(center_x, center_y):\n            return []\n\n        tiles = set()\n        tiles.add((center_x, center_y))\n        current_ring = {(center_x, center_y)}\n\n        for _ in range(radius):\n            next_ring = set()\n            for tile_x, tile_y in current_ring:\n                for neighbor in self.get_neighbors(tile_x, tile_y):\n                    if neighbor not in tiles:\n                        next_ring.add(neighbor)\n                        tiles.add(neighbor)\n            current_ring = next_ring\n\n        return list(tiles)\n\n\nclass Civ6MapParser:\n    \"\"\"Parser for Civ6Map SQLite database files\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, filepath):\n        self.filepath = filepath\n        self.width = 0\n        self.height = 0\n        self.terrain = {}\n        self.features = {}\n        self.resources = {}\n        self.plots = {}\n\n    def parse(self):\n        \"\"\"Parse the SQLite database\"\"\"\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.filepath)\n        cursor = conn.cursor()\n\n        # Get map dimensions from Map table\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT Width, Height FROM Map LIMIT 1\")\n        result = cursor.fetchone()\n        if result:\n            self.width = int(result[0])\n            self.height = int(result[1])\n\n        # Get plot data\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, TerrainType FROM Plots\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, terrain = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.terrain[(x, y)] = terrain if terrain else 'UNKNOWN'\n            self.plots[plot_id] = (x, y)\n\n        # Get features\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, FeatureType FROM PlotFeatures WHERE FeatureType IS NOT NULL\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, feature = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.features[(x, y)] = feature\n\n        # Get resources\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, ResourceType FROM PlotResources WHERE ResourceType IS NOT NULL\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, resource = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.resources[(x, y)] = resource\n\n        conn.close()\n\n    def get_tile_info(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Get all information about a specific tile\"\"\"\n        terrain = self.terrain.get((x, y), 'UNKNOWN')\n        # Check if terrain is a mountain type\n        is_mountain = 'MOUNTAIN' in terrain\n\n        return {\n            'terrain': terrain,\n            'feature': self.features.get((x, y), None),\n            'resource': self.resources.get((x, y), None),\n            'is_mountain': is_mountain\n        }\n\n    def is_valid_tile(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Check if coordinates are within map bounds\"\"\"\n        return 0 <= x < self.width and 0 <= y < self.height\n\n\nclass AdjacencyCalculator:\n    \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonuses for Civ6 districts\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, map_data, hex_grid):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n        self.hex_grid = hex_grid\n\n    def calculate_district_adjacency(self, district_type, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonus for a district at (x, y)\"\"\"\n        if district_type == 'CAMPUS':\n            return self._calc_campus(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'HOLY_SITE':\n            return self._calc_holy_site(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'THEATER_SQUARE':\n            return self._calc_theater_square(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'COMMERCIAL_HUB':\n            return self._calc_commercial_hub(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'HARBOR':\n            return self._calc_harbor(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE':\n            return self._calc_industrial_zone(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n            return self._calc_entertainment_complex(x, y, placements)\n        else:\n            return 0\n\n    def _calc_campus(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Campus: +1 per mountain, +1 per rainforest, +1 per geothermal/reef, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Mountains: +1 (check both terrain and feature)\n            if tile.get('is_mountain') or tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Rainforest/Jungle: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Geothermal Fissure: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_GEOTHERMAL_FISSURE':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Reef: +1 per 2\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_REEF':\n                district_count += 0.5\n\n            # Count other districts (not City Center)\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_holy_site(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Holy Site: +1 per mountain, +1 per 2 woods, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        woods_count = 0\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Mountains: +1 (check both terrain and feature)\n            if tile.get('is_mountain') or tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Woods: +1 per 2\n            if tile.get('feature') in ['FEATURE_FOREST', 'FEATURE_WOODS']:\n                woods_count += 1\n\n            # Count other districts\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += woods_count // 2\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_theater_square(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Theater Square: +1 per 2 districts, +2 per entertainment complex\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n                    bonus += 2\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_commercial_hub(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Commercial Hub: +1 per 2 districts, +2 per harbor on river, +1 per harbor\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'HARBOR':\n                    bonus += 1\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_harbor(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Harbor: +1 per sea resource, +2 per city center, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Sea resources\n            resource = tile.get('resource')\n            terrain = tile.get('terrain')\n            if resource and terrain in ['TERRAIN_COAST', 'TERRAIN_OCEAN']:\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # City center: +2\n            if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] == 'CITY_CENTER':\n                bonus += 2\n\n            # Other districts\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_industrial_zone(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Industrial Zone: +1 per mine/quarry, +2 per aqueduct/dam, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Strategic resources count as mines\n            resource = tile.get('resource')\n            if resource and 'STRATEGIC' in resource:\n                district_count += 0.5\n\n            # Aqueduct bonus\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'AQUEDUCT':\n                    bonus += 2\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_entertainment_complex(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Entertainment Complex: +1 per adjacent entertainment/water park\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district in ['ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX', 'WATER_PARK']:\n                    bonus += 1\n\n        return int(bonus)\n\n\nclass DistrictOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Optimize district placement for maximum adjacency\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, map_data, hex_grid):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n        self.hex_grid = hex_grid\n        self.adjacency_calc = AdjacencyCalculator(map_data, hex_grid)\n\n    def get_max_districts(self, population):\n        \"\"\"Calculate max districts based on population\"\"\"\n        if population < 4:\n            return 0\n        return (population - 1) // 3\n\n    def is_valid_placement(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Check if a tile is valid for district placement\"\"\"\n        if not self.map_data.is_valid_tile(x, y):\n            return False\n\n        # Already occupied\n        if (x, y) in placements:\n            return False\n\n        tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n        terrain = tile.get('terrain', '')\n        feature = tile.get('feature', '')\n\n        # Cannot build on mountains (check terrain type)\n        if tile.get('is_mountain') or feature == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n            return False\n\n        # Cannot build on ocean (except harbor)\n        if terrain == 'TERRAIN_OCEAN':\n            return False\n\n        return True\n\n    def select_districts(self, city_center, max_districts):\n        \"\"\"Intelligently choose which districts to build\"\"\"\n        workable = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n\n        # Count nearby features\n        mountains = 0\n        rainforest = 0\n        water = 0\n        geothermal = 0\n        woods = 0\n\n        for x, y in workable:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n            if tile.get('is_mountain') or tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                mountains += 1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                rainforest += 1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_GEOTHERMAL_FISSURE':\n                geothermal += 1\n            if tile.get('feature') in ['FEATURE_FOREST', 'FEATURE_WOODS']:\n                woods += 1\n            if tile.get('terrain') in ['TERRAIN_COAST', 'TERRAIN_OCEAN']:\n                water += 1\n\n        # Score districts\n        district_scores = {}\n\n        # Campus benefits from mountains, rainforest, and geothermal\n        campus_score = mountains * 3 + rainforest * 2 + geothermal * 2\n        if campus_score > 0:\n            district_scores['CAMPUS'] = campus_score\n\n        # Holy site benefits from mountains and woods\n        holy_score = mountains * 3 + woods\n        if holy_score > 0:\n            district_scores['HOLY_SITE'] = holy_score\n\n        # Harbor if near water\n        if water >= 3:\n            district_scores['HARBOR'] = water\n\n        # Industrial zone benefits from clustering\n        district_scores['INDUSTRIAL_ZONE'] = max_districts * 2\n\n        # Commercial hub\n        district_scores['COMMERCIAL_HUB'] = 5\n\n        # Theater square\n        district_scores['THEATER_SQUARE'] = 3\n\n        # Sort and select top districts\n        sorted_districts = sorted(district_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        selected = [d[0] for d in sorted_districts[:max_districts]]\n\n        # Always ensure we have enough districts\n        all_districts = ['CAMPUS', 'HOLY_SITE', 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE', 'COMMERCIAL_HUB',\n                        'THEATER_SQUARE', 'HARBOR', 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX']\n        for d in all_districts:\n            if len(selected) < max_districts and d not in selected:\n                selected.append(d)\n\n        return selected[:max_districts]\n\n    def optimize_greedy(self, city_center, districts_to_build):\n        \"\"\"Greedy optimization: place each district where it gets best bonus\"\"\"\n        placements = {city_center: 'CITY_CENTER'}\n        workable_tiles = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n\n        # Sort districts by potential benefit\n        for district_type in districts_to_build:\n            best_tile = None\n            best_bonus = -1\n\n            for tile in workable_tiles:\n                if self.is_valid_placement(tile[0], tile[1], placements):\n                    bonus = self.adjacency_calc.calculate_district_adjacency(\n                        district_type, tile[0], tile[1], placements\n                    )\n\n                    if bonus > best_bonus:\n                        best_bonus = bonus\n                        best_tile = tile\n\n            if best_tile:\n                placements[best_tile] = district_type\n\n        return placements\n\n    def optimize_exhaustive(self, city_center, districts_to_build):\n        \"\"\"Try all combinations (for small problems)\"\"\"\n        placements_base = {city_center: 'CITY_CENTER'}\n        workable_tiles = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n        valid_tiles = [t for t in workable_tiles\n                      if self.is_valid_placement(t[0], t[1], placements_base)]\n\n        if len(districts_to_build) > len(valid_tiles):\n            return self.optimize_greedy(city_center, districts_to_build)\n\n        best_solution = None\n        best_score = -1\n\n        # Limit search space\n        max_combinations = 10000\n        tried = 0\n\n        for tiles in combinations(valid_tiles, len(districts_to_build)):\n            if tried >= max_combinations:\n                break\n\n            for district_assignment in permutations(districts_to_build):\n                tried += 1\n                if tried >= max_combinations:\n                    break\n\n                placements = placements_base.copy()\n                for tile, district in zip(tiles, district_assignment):\n                    placements[tile] = district\n\n                score = self.calculate_total_adjacency(placements)\n\n                if score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_solution = placements\n\n        return best_solution if best_solution else self.optimize_greedy(city_center, districts_to_build)\n\n    def calculate_total_adjacency(self, placements):\n        \"\"\"Calculate total adjacency for all districts\"\"\"\n        total = 0\n        for (x, y), district in placements.items():\n            if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                bonus = self.adjacency_calc.calculate_district_adjacency(district, x, y, placements)\n                total += bonus\n        return total\n\n    def find_best_city_location(self, max_districts):\n        \"\"\"Find optimal city center location\"\"\"\n        best_location = None\n        best_score = -1\n\n        # Try different city center locations\n        for y in range(self.map_data.height):\n            for x in range(self.map_data.width):\n                if not self.map_data.is_valid_tile(x, y):\n                    continue\n\n                tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n                if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                    continue\n                if tile.get('terrain') == 'TERRAIN_OCEAN':\n                    continue\n\n                # Quick heuristic: count nearby mountains\n                workable = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(x, y, 3)\n                mountain_count = sum(1 for wx, wy in workable\n                                   if self.map_data.get_tile_info(wx, wy).get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS')\n\n                if mountain_count > best_score:\n                    best_score = mountain_count\n                    best_location = (x, y)\n\n        return best_location if best_location else (self.map_data.width // 2, self.map_data.height // 2)\n\n\ndef solve_scenario(scenario_path, output_path):\n    \"\"\"Main solver function\"\"\"\n    # Load scenario\n    with open(scenario_path, 'r') as f:\n        scenario = json.load(f)\n\n    # Parse map\n    map_path = f\"/data/{scenario['map_file']}\"\n    map_data = Civ6MapParser(map_path)\n    map_data.parse()\n\n    print(f\"Map size: {map_data.width}x{map_data.height}\")\n\n    # Create hex grid\n    hex_grid = HexGrid(map_data.width, map_data.height)\n\n    # Create optimizer\n    optimizer = DistrictOptimizer(map_data, hex_grid)\n\n    # Get constraints\n    num_cities = scenario['num_cities']\n    population = scenario['population']\n    max_districts = optimizer.get_max_districts(population)\n\n    print(f\"Population: {population}, Max districts: {max_districts}\")\n\n    # Find best city center location\n    city_center = optimizer.find_best_city_location(max_districts)\n    print(f\"City center: {city_center}\")\n\n    # Select districts to build\n    districts = optimizer.select_districts(city_center, max_districts)\n    print(f\"Districts to build: {districts}\")\n\n    # Optimize placement\n    if max_districts <= 3:\n        placements = optimizer.optimize_exhaustive(city_center, districts)\n    else:\n        placements = optimizer.optimize_greedy(city_center, districts)\n\n    # Even column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, -1), (1, 0), (0, 1), (-1, 0), (-1, -1)]\n        else:  # Odd column\n            offsets = [(0, -1), (1, 0), (1, 1), (0, 1), (-1, 1), (-1, 0)]\n\n        neighbors = []\n        for dx, dy in offsets:\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            if self.is_valid(nx, ny):\n                neighbors.append((nx, ny))\n        return neighbors\n\n    def get_tiles_in_range(self, center_x, center_y, radius):\n        \"\"\"Get all tiles within radius of center using BFS\"\"\"\n        if not self.is_valid(center_x, center_y):\n            return []\n\n        tiles = set()\n        tiles.add((center_x, center_y))\n        current_ring = {(center_x, center_y)}\n\n        for _ in range(radius):\n            next_ring = set()\n            for tile_x, tile_y in current_ring:\n                for neighbor in self.get_neighbors(tile_x, tile_y):\n                    if neighbor not in tiles:\n                        next_ring.add(neighbor)\n                        tiles.add(neighbor)\n            current_ring = next_ring\n\n        return list(tiles)\n\n\nclass Civ6MapParser:\n    \"\"\"Parser for Civ6Map SQLite database files\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, filepath):\n        self.filepath = filepath\n        self.width = 0\n        self.height = 0\n        self.terrain = {}\n        self.features = {}\n        self.resources = {}\n        self.plots = {}\n\n    def parse(self):\n        \"\"\"Parse the SQLite database\"\"\"\n        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.filepath)\n        cursor = conn.cursor()\n\n        # Get map dimensions from Map table\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT Width, Height FROM Map LIMIT 1\")\n        result = cursor.fetchone()\n        if result:\n            self.width = int(result[0])\n            self.height = int(result[1])\n\n        # Get plot data\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, TerrainType FROM Plots\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, terrain = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.terrain[(x, y)] = terrain if terrain else 'UNKNOWN'\n            self.plots[plot_id] = (x, y)\n\n        # Get features\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, FeatureType FROM PlotFeatures WHERE FeatureType IS NOT NULL\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, feature = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.features[(x, y)] = feature\n\n        # Get resources\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, ResourceType FROM PlotResources WHERE ResourceType IS NOT NULL\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, resource = row\n            x = plot_id % self.width\n            y = plot_id // self.width\n            self.resources[(x, y)] = resource\n\n        conn.close()\n\n    def get_tile_info(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Get all information about a specific tile\"\"\"\n        terrain = self.terrain.get((x, y), 'UNKNOWN')\n        # Check if terrain is a mountain type\n        is_mountain = 'MOUNTAIN' in terrain\n\n        return {\n            'terrain': terrain,\n            'feature': self.features.get((x, y), None),\n            'resource': self.resources.get((x, y), None),\n            'is_mountain': is_mountain\n        }\n\n    def is_valid_tile(self, x, y):\n        \"\"\"Check if coordinates are within map bounds\"\"\"\n        return 0 <= x < self.width and 0 <= y < self.height\n\n\nclass AdjacencyCalculator:\n    \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonuses for Civ6 districts\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, map_data, hex_grid):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n        self.hex_grid = hex_grid\n\n    def calculate_district_adjacency(self, district_type, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonus for a district at (x, y)\"\"\"\n        if district_type == 'CAMPUS':\n            return self._calc_campus(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'HOLY_SITE':\n            return self._calc_holy_site(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'THEATER_SQUARE':\n            return self._calc_theater_square(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'COMMERCIAL_HUB':\n            return self._calc_commercial_hub(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'HARBOR':\n            return self._calc_harbor(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE':\n            return self._calc_industrial_zone(x, y, placements)\n        elif district_type == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n            return self._calc_entertainment_complex(x, y, placements)\n        else:\n            return 0\n\n    def _calc_campus(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Campus: +1 per mountain, +1 per rainforest, +1 per geothermal/reef, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Mountains: +1 (check both terrain and feature)\n            if tile.get('is_mountain') or tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Rainforest/Jungle: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Geothermal Fissure: +1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_GEOTHERMAL_FISSURE':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Reef: +1 per 2\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_REEF':\n                district_count += 0.5\n\n            # Count other districts (not City Center)\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_holy_site(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Holy Site: +1 per mountain, +1 per 2 woods, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        woods_count = 0\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Mountains: +1 (check both terrain and feature)\n            if tile.get('is_mountain') or tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # Woods: +1 per 2\n            if tile.get('feature') in ['FEATURE_FOREST', 'FEATURE_WOODS']:\n                woods_count += 1\n\n            # Count other districts\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += woods_count // 2\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_theater_square(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Theater Square: +1 per 2 districts, +2 per entertainment complex\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n                    bonus += 2\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_commercial_hub(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Commercial Hub: +1 per 2 districts, +2 per harbor on river, +1 per harbor\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'HARBOR':\n                    bonus += 1\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_harbor(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Harbor: +1 per sea resource, +2 per city center, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Sea resources\n            resource = tile.get('resource')\n            terrain = tile.get('terrain')\n            if resource and terrain in ['TERRAIN_COAST', 'TERRAIN_OCEAN']:\n                bonus += 1\n\n            # City center: +2\n            if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] == 'CITY_CENTER':\n                bonus += 2\n\n            # Other districts\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_industrial_zone(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Industrial Zone: +1 per mine/quarry, +2 per aqueduct/dam, +1 per 2 districts\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n        district_count = 0\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(nx, ny)\n\n            # Strategic resources count as mines\n            resource = tile.get('resource')\n            if resource and 'STRATEGIC' in resource:\n                district_count += 0.5\n\n            # Aqueduct bonus\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district == 'AQUEDUCT':\n                    bonus += 2\n                elif district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n\n        bonus += district_count // 2\n        return int(bonus)\n\n    def _calc_entertainment_complex(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Entertainment Complex: +1 per adjacent entertainment/water park\"\"\"\n        bonus = 0\n        neighbors = self.hex_grid.get_neighbors(x, y)\n\n        for nx, ny in neighbors:\n            if (nx, ny) in placements:\n                district = placements[(nx, ny)]\n                if district in ['ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX', 'WATER_PARK']:\n                    bonus += 1\n\n        return int(bonus)\n\n\nclass DistrictOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Optimize district placement for maximum adjacency\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, map_data, hex_grid):\n        self.map_data = map_data\n        self.hex_grid = hex_grid\n        self.adjacency_calc = AdjacencyCalculator(map_data, hex_grid)\n\n    def get_max_districts(self, population):\n        \"\"\"Calculate max districts based on population\"\"\"\n        if population < 4:\n            return 0\n        return (population - 1) // 3\n\n    def is_valid_placement(self, x, y, placements):\n        \"\"\"Check if a tile is valid for district placement\"\"\"\n        if not self.map_data.is_valid_tile(x, y):\n            return False\n\n        # Already occupied\n        if (x, y) in placements:\n            return False\n\n        tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n        terrain = tile.get('terrain', '')\n        feature = tile.get('feature', '')\n\n        # Cannot build on mountains (check terrain type)\n        if tile.get('is_mountain') or feature == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n            return False\n\n        # Cannot build on ocean (except harbor)\n        if terrain == 'TERRAIN_OCEAN':\n            return False\n\n        return True\n\n    def select_districts(self, city_center, max_districts):\n        \"\"\"Intelligently choose which districts to build\"\"\"\n        workable = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n\n        # Count nearby features\n        mountains = 0\n        rainforest = 0\n        water = 0\n        geothermal = 0\n        woods = 0\n\n        for x, y in workable:\n            tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n            if tile.get('is_mountain') or tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                mountains += 1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                rainforest += 1\n            if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_GEOTHERMAL_FISSURE':\n                geothermal += 1\n            if tile.get('feature') in ['FEATURE_FOREST', 'FEATURE_WOODS']:\n                woods += 1\n            if tile.get('terrain') in ['TERRAIN_COAST', 'TERRAIN_OCEAN']:\n                water += 1\n\n        # Score districts\n        district_scores = {}\n\n        # Campus benefits from mountains, rainforest, and geothermal\n        campus_score = mountains * 3 + rainforest * 2 + geothermal * 2\n        if campus_score > 0:\n            district_scores['CAMPUS'] = campus_score\n\n        # Holy site benefits from mountains and woods\n        holy_score = mountains * 3 + woods\n        if holy_score > 0:\n            district_scores['HOLY_SITE'] = holy_score\n\n        # Harbor if near water\n        if water >= 3:\n            district_scores['HARBOR'] = water\n\n        # Industrial zone benefits from clustering\n        district_scores['INDUSTRIAL_ZONE'] = max_districts * 2\n\n        # Commercial hub\n        district_scores['COMMERCIAL_HUB'] = 5\n\n        # Theater square\n        district_scores['THEATER_SQUARE'] = 3\n\n        # Sort and select top districts\n        sorted_districts = sorted(district_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        selected = [d[0] for d in sorted_districts[:max_districts]]\n\n        # Always ensure we have enough districts\n        all_districts = ['CAMPUS', 'HOLY_SITE', 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE', 'COMMERCIAL_HUB',\n                        'THEATER_SQUARE', 'HARBOR', 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX']\n        for d in all_districts:\n            if len(selected) < max_districts and d not in selected:\n                selected.append(d)\n\n        return selected[:max_districts]\n\n    def optimize_greedy(self, city_center, districts_to_build):\n        \"\"\"Greedy optimization: place each district where it gets best bonus\"\"\"\n        placements = {city_center: 'CITY_CENTER'}\n        workable_tiles = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n\n        # Sort districts by potential benefit\n        for district_type in districts_to_build:\n            best_tile = None\n            best_bonus = -1\n\n            for tile in workable_tiles:\n                if self.is_valid_placement(tile[0], tile[1], placements):\n                    bonus = self.adjacency_calc.calculate_district_adjacency(\n                        district_type, tile[0], tile[1], placements\n                    )\n\n                    if bonus > best_bonus:\n                        best_bonus = bonus\n                        best_tile = tile\n\n            if best_tile:\n                placements[best_tile] = district_type\n\n        return placements\n\n    def optimize_exhaustive(self, city_center, districts_to_build):\n        \"\"\"Try all combinations (for small problems)\"\"\"\n        placements_base = {city_center: 'CITY_CENTER'}\n        workable_tiles = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(city_center[0], city_center[1], 3)\n        valid_tiles = [t for t in workable_tiles\n                      if self.is_valid_placement(t[0], t[1], placements_base)]\n\n        if len(districts_to_build) > len(valid_tiles):\n            return self.optimize_greedy(city_center, districts_to_build)\n\n        best_solution = None\n        best_score = -1\n\n        # Limit search space\n        max_combinations = 10000\n        tried = 0\n\n        for tiles in combinations(valid_tiles, len(districts_to_build)):\n            if tried >= max_combinations:\n                break\n\n            for district_assignment in permutations(districts_to_build):\n                tried += 1\n                if tried >= max_combinations:\n                    break\n\n                placements = placements_base.copy()\n                for tile, district in zip(tiles, district_assignment):\n                    placements[tile] = district\n\n                score = self.calculate_total_adjacency(placements)\n\n                if score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_solution = placements\n\n        return best_solution if best_solution else self.optimize_greedy(city_center, districts_to_build)\n\n    def calculate_total_adjacency(self, placements):\n        \"\"\"Calculate total adjacency for all districts\"\"\"\n        total = 0\n        for (x, y), district in placements.items():\n            if district != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                bonus = self.adjacency_calc.calculate_district_adjacency(district, x, y, placements)\n                total += bonus\n        return total\n\n    def find_best_city_location(self, max_districts):\n        \"\"\"Find optimal city center location\"\"\"\n        best_location = None\n        best_score = -1\n\n        # Try different city center locations\n        for y in range(self.map_data.height):\n            for x in range(self.map_data.width):\n                if not self.map_data.is_valid_tile(x, y):\n                    continue\n\n                tile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(x, y)\n                if tile.get('is_mountain') or tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n                    continue\n                if tile.get('terrain') == 'TERRAIN_OCEAN':\n                    continue\n\n                # Heuristic: count nearby valuable features\n                workable = self.hex_grid.get_tiles_in_range(x, y, 3)\n                score = 0\n                for wx, wy in workable:\n                    wtile = self.map_data.get_tile_info(wx, wy)\n                    if wtile.get('is_mountain'):\n                        score += 5\n                    if wtile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                        score += 3\n                    if wtile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_GEOTHERMAL_FISSURE':\n                        score += 3\n                    if wtile.get('feature') in ['FEATURE_FOREST', 'FEATURE_WOODS']:\n                        score += 1\n\n                if score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_location = (x, y)\n\n        return best_location if best_location else (self.map_data.width // 2, self.map_data.height // 2)\n\n\ndef solve_scenario(scenario_path, output_path):\n    \"\"\"Main solver function\"\"\"\n    #
